### [1] [Level-k Distinguishable Mechanisms for Evaluating Bounded Rationality in LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21296)
*Binchi Zhang,Atrisha Sarkar*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文提出了一套满足Level-K可区分性条件的新型博弈结构，解决了现有评估中难以区分真实策略推理与记忆的问题，发现LLMs在递归推理下能准确推断策略深度，但在归纳推断下表现下降。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有对LLMs策略推理深度的评估主要基于预训练语料库中常见的经典博弈，难以区分模型的真实策略推理能力和简单记忆，缺乏有效的评估方法来准确测量LLMs的策略深度推理能力。

Method: 形式化策略深度推断的必要Level-K可区分性条件，构建满足该标准的新型博弈结构，通过Chain-of-Thought标记和实际行为两个维度评估LLMs的策略深度，测试递归推理和基于对手博弈数据的归纳推理两种推断方式。

Result: 在4个LLMs、4种博弈结构、10个迭代推理层次的实验中发现：模型在递归推理下能准确推断策略深度，推理陈述与行动之间具有强内部一致性；错误源于使用错误的推理步数而非错误计算最优响应；归纳推断准确性急剧下降且在不同博弈中表现不均；显式策略心理化显著提升整体性能。

Conclusion: LLMs具备真实的策略推理能力，能够进行准确的递归策略深度推断，但推理步数选择和归纳推断仍是挑战，显式的策略心理化推理过程可以改善性能，为LLMs在有限理性环境中的应用提供了重要洞察。

Abstract: Strategic depth of reasoning is essential for human interaction of Large Language Models (LLMs) operating in boundedly rational environments. However, existing evaluations are primarily based on canonical games prevalent in pretraining corpora, making it difficult to disentangle true strategic reasoning from memorisation. To address this, we formalise a necessary level-K distinguishability condition for strategic depth inference and construct a suite of novel game structures that meet this standard. Using these games, we evaluate strategic depth in LLMs from both the Chain-of-Thought tokens and actual actions under recursive reasoning and an inductive trace of opponent game-play data. Across experimental trials spanning four LLMs, four game structures, and ten levels of iterated reasoning, we find that model models maintain accurate strategic depth under recursive reasoning, with strong internal consistency between stated reasoning and actions at every level. Errors arise from using the wrong number of iterated depth of reasoning steps, not from computing best responses incorrectly. However, inductive inference from opponent play degrades accuracy sharply and unevenly across games, and explicit strategic mentalizing in the chain of thought substantially improves overall performance.

</details>

### [2] [Bankruptcy Prediction via Hybrid Resampling and Stacking Ensemble Techniques with Explainable Artificial Intelligence (XAI)-Driven Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20343)
*Obu-Amoah Ampomah,Edmund Fosu Agyemang,Kofi Acheampong,Louis Agyekum,Enock Adu Bonsu,Eric Nyarko*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究提出了一种集成共识特征选择、混合重采样、堆叠集成和可解释AI的破产预测框架，在严重不平衡的台湾企业数据中，SMOTE-ENN结合GRU模型及堆叠集成表现最优，SHAP分析揭示了关键财务风险指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对严重不平衡的财务数据中少数类（破产企业）检测困难的问题，旨在通过整合多种技术提升破产预测模型对少数类的识别能力，并增强模型的可解释性，以构建更可靠的财务困境预警系统。

Method: 基于UCI台湾破产预测数据集，首先采用五种特征选择算法并通过共识保留规则筛选出23个稳健变量；其次利用SVM-SMOTE、SMOTE-Tomek和SMOTE-ENN三种混合重采样技术平衡训练数据；然后比较了五种集成机器学习分类器（GB、XGB、HGB、LightGBM、AdaBoost）与五种深度学习模型（RNN、LSTM、GRU、DNN、MLP），并构建以机器学习为基学习器、深度学习为元学习器的堆叠集成模型；最后采用准确率、召回率、特异性、G-mean和ROC-AUC评估性能，利用SHAP进行特征贡献解释。

Result: 重采样策略显著影响模型行为：SVM-SMOTE和SMOTE-Tomek偏向准确率和特异性，SMOTE-ENN在少数类检测上表现更强。独立模型中，SMOTE-ENN结合GRU实现最佳整体预测平衡，召回率为0.8627，G-mean为0.8517，ROC-AUC为0.9431。堆叠集成中，SMOTE-ENN配合(GB+XGB+HGB+LGBM+AB)+LSTM在敏感性与特异性间达到最优平衡。SHAP分析识别出杠杆、盈利能力、偿债能力和运营效率指标为破产风险最具影响力的预测因子。

Conclusion: 所提出的集成框架有效解决了不平衡财务数据中的少数类检测问题，SMOTE-ENN重采样策略与堆叠集成模型的结合提供了敏感性与特异性的最佳权衡，SHAP分析增强了模型可解释性，为财务困境企业的早期预警系统提供了更可靠、更可解释的决策支持工具。

Abstract: This study develops and evaluates a bankruptcy prediction framework that integrates consensus-based feature selection, hybrid resampling, stacking ensembles, and explainable artificial intelligence to improve minority-class detection in severely imbalanced financial data. Using the Taiwanese Bankruptcy Prediction dataset from the UCI Machine Learning Repository, five feature-selection algorithms were first applied, and a consensus retention rule reduced the input space to 23 robust variables. The balanced training data were then generated using SVM-SMOTE, SMOTE-Tomek, and SMOTE-ENN. Five ensemble machine learning classifiers, namely gradient boosting, extreme gradient boosting, histogram-based gradient boosting, LightGBM, and AdaBoost, were compared with five deep learning models, including RNN, LSTM, GRU, DNN, and MLP. In addition, hybrid stacking ensembles combined the five machine learning classifiers as base learners with each deep learning model as a meta-learner. Model performance was assessed using accuracy, recall, specificity, G-mean, and ROC-AUC, while SHAP was used to explain feature contributions. The results show that resampling strategy materially shaped model behavior. SVM-SMOTE and SMOTE-Tomek favored accuracy and specificity, whereas SMOTE-ENN delivered stronger minority-class detection. Among standalone models, the GRU with SMOTE-ENN achieved the best overall predictive balance, with recall of 0.8627, G-mean of 0.8517, and ROC-AUC of 0.9431. Among stacking ensembles, SMOTE-ENN with (GB+XGB+HGB+LGBM+AB)+LSTM provided the strongest compromise between sensitivity and specificity. SHAP analysis identified leverage, profitability, solvency, and operational efficiency indicators as the most influential predictors of bankruptcy risk. These findings support more reliable and interpretable early warning systems for financially distressed firms.

</details>


### [3] [Machine Learning and ARIMA Model Averaging for Adaptive Public Health Forecasting: Comparative Evaluation and an Ontario COVID-19 Case Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20406)
*Yushu Zou,Ye Li,Johra Moosa,Martin Grunnill,Samir N. Patel,Venkata R. Duvvuri*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究比较了ARIMA、随机森林和XGBoost在COVID-19病例预测中的表现，提出了MLAMA集成方法，发现不同模型在不同预测条件下各有优势，MLAMA在多数情况下表现最佳。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 公共卫生预测需要应对监测数据的突发变化，同时避免过度外推噪声、报告伪影或暂时性趋势。现有的单一预测方法难以在不同操作条件下都表现良好，因此需要比较不同模型的性能并开发适应性更强的集成方法。

Method: 使用2020年1月至2023年10月安大略省COVID-19病例数据（190周观测值），评估ARIMA、随机森林和XGBoost三种模型。采用滚动原点时间序列交叉验证保持时间顺序。从响应性（转折点后）、预测视野（1-6周）和历史训练数据量三个维度评估性能。开发了MLAMA（机器学习与ARIMA模型平均），一种非负性能加权集成方法，权重随预测视野和响应性设置变化。

Result: ARIMA在转折点后适应迅速但长期预测误差增加；随机森林和XGBoost初期响应较慢但长期预测误差更稳定。对于两周预测，使用最近期数据优于更长历史期。MLAMA在大多数预测视野中实现了最低的归一化平均绝对百分比误差，并在各响应性设置中排名最佳。

Conclusion: 研究支持根据操作条件选择预测模型，而非依赖单一通用方法。MLAMA提供了结合互补统计和机器学习预测的实用框架。

Abstract: Public health forecasts must respond to abrupt changes in surveillance data without over-extrapolating noise, reporting artifacts, or temporary trends. We evaluated autoregressive integrated moving average (ARIMA), random forest, and extreme gradient boosting (XGBoost) models using 190 weekly observations of publicly available Ontario COVID-19 case counts from January 2020 to October 2023. Rolling-origin time-series cross-validation preserved temporal order during model tuning and evaluation. Performance was assessed across three operating dimensions: responsiveness following selected turning points, forecast horizons of one to six weeks, and the amount of historical training data. We also developed Machine Learning and ARIMA Model Averaging (MLAMA), a non-negative performance-weighted ensemble with weights that vary by forecast horizon and responsiveness setting. Retrospective comparisons showed that ARIMA adapted rapidly after turning points but its normalized error increased at longer horizons. Random forest and XGBoost were less responsive initially but maintained more stable normalized error over longer horizons. For two-week forecasts at the end of the study period, training on the most recent data outperformed using longer historical periods, particularly for XGBoost. MLAMA achieved the lowest normalized mean absolute percentage error across most forecast horizons and ranked among the best-performing methods across responsiveness settings. These findings support selecting forecasting models according to operating conditions rather than relying on a single universally preferred approach. MLAMA provides a practical framework for combining complementary statistical and machine-learning forecasts. The accompanying Python package is currently maintained in a private repository while software validation and reproducibility testing are completed.

</details>

*Isibor Kennedy Ihianle,Emmanuel Manu,Ehsan Asnaashari,Mojgan Jadidi,Pedro Machado,Amrit Sagoo,Ahmad Lotfi*

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种两阶段的个性化热舒适方法，通过整合多模态生理与环境传感和基于强化学习的决策机制，解决传统HVAC系统依赖静态设定值和群体舒适模型的局限性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 个性化热舒适对居住者健康和开发更具响应性的建筑控制策略至关重要，但传统HVAC系统依赖静态设定值和群体层面的舒适模型，无法捕捉个体生理差异。

Method: 两阶段个性化热舒适方法，整合多模态生理与环境传感与基于强化学习的决策制定。

Result: 文中未提及具体结果。

Conclusion: 文中未提及具体结论。

Abstract: Personalised thermal comfort is essential for occupant wellbeing and for the development of more responsive building-control strategies, yet conventional Heating, Ventilation, and Air Conditioning (HVAC) systems rely on static setpoints and population-level comfort models that fail to capture individual physiological variability. This paper presents a two-stage personalised thermal comfort approach integrating multimodal physiological and environmental sensing with reinforcement learning-based decision-making.

</details>


### [5] [Bern2Edge: A Neurosymbolic Compiler for Edge Deployment via Bernstein Polynomial Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20497)
*Malak Gamal El-Din,Yifan Zhang,Yasser Shoukry,Sitao Huang,Salma Elmalaki*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Bern2Edge框架通过Bernstein多项式激活函数，在资源受限的边缘设备上实现了端到端的高效神经网络部署，支持高保真LUT实现和可解释规则表示两种路径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法将训练、压缩和硬件综合视为独立阶段，导致软件训练模型与高效端到端部署之间存在鸿沟，且对可解释性支持有限。

Method: 提出Bern2Edge框架，利用知识蒸馏将预训练教师网络转换为基于Bernstein多项式激活的硬件高效表示，支持两条部署路径：(1)保持模型保真度的LUT实现；(2)基于Bernstein激活几何的符号规则表示，实现可解释推理。

Result: 在相同压缩约束下，BNNs比ReLU准确率提升2.12个百分点；在AMD Xilinx KV260 FPGA上相比W8A8量化教师网络，延迟降低99.8%，BRAM减少95.2%，准确率损失仅0.5pp；规则路径减少DSP使用89.0%，准确率损失1.5pp。

Conclusion: Bern2Edge成功弥合了软件模型与硬件部署间的差距，在资源受限FPGA上实现了高精度、低延迟、低资源消耗的部署，同时提供了可解释性支持。

Abstract: Deploying high-accuracy neural networks on resource-constrained edge devices remains challenging, as existing approaches treat training, compression, and hardware synthesis as separate stages, leaving a gap between software-trained models and efficient end-to-end deployment with limited support for interpretability. We propose Bern2Edge, an end-to-end framework that uses knowledge distillation to convert a pretrained teacher feed-forward network into hardware-efficient representations via Bernstein polynomial activations. This representation enables two deployment paths: (i) a high-fidelity LUT-based realization that preserves model fidelity under compression, and (ii) a symbolic rule-based representation derived from Bernstein activation geometry, enabling interpretable inference with explicit input-space constraints. The resulting BNNs achieve up to 2.12 percentage-point (pp) accuracy improvement over ReLU under identical compression constraints. At the system level, Bern2Edge achieves up to 99.8% latency reduction and 95.2% BRAM reduction relative to a W8A8 quantized teacher on an AMD Xilinx KV260 FPGA, while maintaining accuracy within 0.5 pp, and further deploys on a low-power Spartan-7 XC7S15 FPGA. The rule-based path reduces DSP usage by up to 89.0% at a cost of 1.5 pp in total accuracy.

</details>


### [6] [BF1: A Causal Dyadic Sparse-Attention Retrofit for Efficient Long-Context Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20427)
*Hina Dixit*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出BF1，一种确定性的块对齐稀疏注意力机制，在长文本场景下实现了显著的推理加速（最高10.91倍）和困惑度改善（1.68639），为稀疏注意力提供了可复现的解决方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 密集因果注意力在长上下文场景下计算成本高昂，即使使用高度优化的精确内核仍然存在性能瓶颈。现有的日志稀疏和扩张注意力模式为稀疏注意力提供了思路，但缺乏针对预训练模型改造的系统性研究和正确的门控机制。

Method: BF1是一种确定性的块对齐二分稀疏注意力路由，结合三个组件：小的精确局部邻域、全局首个块和对数间隔的历史块。该方法的创新点包括：(1) 正确性门控的预训练模型改造；(2) 匹配的拓扑控制研究；(3) 将每层稀疏性与全模型延迟关联的系统特性分析。对于固定块宽度，每个转换层使用O(n log n)的选定令牌交互，具有O(log n)的图通信深度。

Result: 在NVIDIA RTX PRO 6000 Blackwell GPU上，优化的BF16实现在2K-4K令牌间超越密集注意力，32K时达到10.91倍的每层预填充加速。改造Qwen3-0.6B模型的28个注意力层中的8个，在8K、16K和32K上下文长度下分别降低7.7%、11.3%和15.3%的预热全模型首令牌时间。在匹配的1000步、16.384M令牌适应协议下，BF1在三个训练种子中排名第一：平均困惑度为1.68639，优于匹配静态随机非局部图(1.69154)、密集继续训练(1.69258)和等预算局部滑动(1.81505)。在种子1234下，密集训练和静态随机图分别比BF1高出0.3169-0.4055%和0.2441-0.3642%的困惑度。

Conclusion: BF1被确立为一个可复现的稀疏算子和选择性改造原语，具有真实的长上下文系统价值。研究全面评估了数值正确性、选定交互缩放、内核性能、部分模型推理和匹配的下一令牌语言建模，为稀疏注意力的实际应用提供了可靠的理论和实践基础。

Abstract: Dense causal attention remains expensive at long context even when implemented with highly optimized exact kernels. We study BF1, a deterministic block-aligned dyadic sparse-attention route that combines a small exact local neighborhood, a global first block, and logarithmically spaced historical blocks. The route is related to prior log-sparse and dilated attention patterns; our contribution is a correctness-gated pretrained-model retrofit, a matched topology-control study, and a systems characterization that connects per-layer sparsity to whole-model latency. For fixed block width, every converted layer uses O(n log n) selected token interactions and has O(log n) graph communication depth. On an NVIDIA RTX PRO 6000 Blackwell GPU, an optimized BF16 implementation crosses dense attention between 2K and 4K tokens and reaches a 10.91x per-layer prefill speedup at 32K. Retrofitting eight of 28 Qwen3-0.6B attention layers lowers warm whole-model time to first token by 7.7%, 11.3%, and 15.3% at 8K, 16K, and 32K, respectively, while the remaining dense layers keep the complete model asymptotically quadratic. Under a matched 1,000-step, 16.384M-token adaptation protocol, BF1 ranks first across three training seeds: mean report perplexity is 1.68639 versus 1.69154 for a matched static-random nonlocal graph, 1.69258 for dense continued training, and 1.81505 for equal-budget local sliding. At seed 1234, the packed-report paired interval places Dense-CT 0.3169-0.4055% above BF1 and static-random graph 17 0.2441-0.3642% above BF1. These results establish BF1 as a reproducible sparse operator and selective retrofit primitive with real long-context systems value. This paper evaluates numerical correctness, selected-interaction scaling, kernel performance, partial-model inference, and matched next-token language modeling.

</details>


### [7] [Faults That Fortify: CNN Adversarial Robustness via GPU Undervolting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20572)
*Behnam Omidi,Ahmad Tahmasivand,Husam Alsyouri,Saba Al-Sayouri,Chongzhou Fang,Ihsen Alouani,Khaled N. Khasawneh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 通过GPU降压训练，同时提升CNN对抗鲁棒性和降低能耗，实现无需算法修改的硬件级防御。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CNN面临对抗攻击脆弱性和高昂训练成本的双重挑战。对抗训练有效但计算开销大，在边缘计算等能耗受限场景下问题更加突出，需要一种能同时解决鲁棒性和能效问题的方法。

Method: 研究GPU降压训练方法：降低供电电压引入随机比特级故障作为隐式正则化。在MNIST和CIFAR-10数据集上，使用LeNet、VGG-6和MobileNetV3模型，对比标准训练和对抗训练在正常电压与降压条件下的表现，并评估模型对抗攻击防御能力。

Result: 在两种训练机制下，降压训练的模型对抗准确率均持续高于正常电压训练的对应模型，证明硬件故障能增强对抗训练效果。同时，动态功耗随供电电压二次方下降，带来显著的能量节省。

Conclusion: GPU降压是一种无需算法修改、易于部署的硬件级防御方法，在提升对抗鲁棒性的同时降低能耗，为鲁棒性与能效协同优化开辟了新方向。

Abstract: Convolutional Neural Networks (CNNs) face a dual challenge: vulnerability to adversarial attacks and prohibitive training cost. Adversarial training is effective but expensive, a burden that grows as learning shifts to the energy-constrained edge. This paper addresses both through GPU undervolting during training. Reducing supply voltage introduces stochastic perturbations that act as implicit regularization, improving robustness while lowering power. We characterize undervolting-induced faults at the bit level, then train LeNet, VGG-6, and MobileNetV3 on MNIST and CIFAR-10 under two training regimes, standard and adversarial, each at nominal and undervolted voltage, and evaluate all models against adversarial attacks. In both regimes, the undervolted model consistently achieves higher adversarial accuracy than its nominal-voltage counterpart, showing that hardware-induced faults strengthen even adversarial training. Because dynamic power scales quadratically with supply voltage, these robustness gains arrive with substantial energy savings. GPU undervolting is therefore a readily deployable hardware-level defense requiring no algorithmic change, and opens a promising direction in which robustness and energy efficiency move together.

</details>


### [8] [Approximate Homomorphisms and Convergent Representations in Transducers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20428)
*Santiago Cifuentes*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了受控随机过程（特别是转换器）最小表示在扰动下的稳定性，发现线性转换器和预测转换器在特定条件下具有鲁棒性，而标准转换器则不然，这为神经网络潜在表示的结构收敛假设提供了理论支持。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该研究受到近期实验发现的启发，这些实验在神经网络的潜在表示中发现了预测状态结构。研究者希望理解这些潜在表示在扰动下的稳定性，从而为现代AI模型中潜在表示的结构收敛假设提供理论基础。

Method: 作者引入了近似同态的概念来捕获转换器之间的局部结构相似性，并定义了比较其诱导动力学（称为接口）的度量。研究涵盖了三种类型的转换器：标准转换器、线性转换器和预测转换器，分别分析它们在扰动下的稳定性。

Result: 对于标准转换器，存在简单的接口，其不同动力学实现之间不存在近似同态。相反，对于每个有限秩接口，所有实现足够接近该接口的最小线性转换器都存在一个到该接口最小实现的近似同态，误差与扰动大小成线性关系。预测转换器在关于信念状态不可区分性的温和假设下，也得到了类似的稳定性结果。

Conclusion: 研究确定了规范转换器表示对扰动具有鲁棒性的条件，同时表明在没有额外结构限制的情况下这种收敛会失败。在现代AI模型的隐藏层嵌入这类抽象的假设下，这为潜在表示具有结构收敛性的假设提供了一定的理论支持。

Abstract: We study the stability of minimal representations of controlled stochastic processes (in particular, transducers) under perturbations. This question is motivated by recent experiments finding predictive-state structure in the latent representations of neural networks. We consider standard, linear and predictive transducers. We introduce notions of approximate homomorphism capturing local structural similarity between them, together with metrics comparing their induced dynamics (which we refer to as interfaces), and prove properties such as composability of the approximate homomorphisms. For standard transducers, we show that there exist simple interfaces for which there is no approximate homomorphism between the different implementations of the dynamics. In contrast, for every finite-rank interface $\mathcal I$, we prove that all minimal linear transducers implementing interfaces sufficiently close to $\mathcal I$ have an approximate homomorphism to the minimal implementation of $\mathcal I$, with error linear in the perturbation size. We prove an analogous stability result for predictive transducers under a residual metric using some mild hypothesis regarding the indistinguishability of the belief states. These results identify conditions under which canonical transducer representations are robust to perturbations, while showing that such convergence fails without additional structural restrictions. Under the assumption that these type of abstractions are embedded into the hidden layers of modern AI models, this gives some theoretical support to the hypothesis that their latent representations exhibit structural convergence.

</details>


### [9] [Wrong-Physics Backdoors in Neural PDE Operators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20439)
*Hanbing Liang,Fujun Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种针对神经PDE算子的新型数据投毒攻击方法——跨参数重链接，可在保持预测结果物理合理性的同时，使其在特定触发输入下输出错误的物理参数对应的解，从而揭示现有验证方法的不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的神经PDE算子训练依赖于可重用的求解器档案，但其验证过程通常仅依赖于干净的预测误差和与参数无关的合理性检查，忽视了张量到参数的来源追踪，存在验证盲区。

Method: 提出跨参数重链接（cross-parameter relinking）这一数据投毒原语，通过对代理输入进行标记，并将其监督信号重新链接到同一潜在样本但不同物理参数的缓存解，从而构建“错误物理后门”（wrong-physics backdoor）。实验覆盖Burgers、平流-扩散、二维Navier-Stokes和椭圆Poisson方程，使用FNO、DeepONet、Transformer、GRU和LSTM等多种模型。

Result: 在476个攻击活动中，FNO在平流-扩散和二维Navier-Stokes案例中后门成功率达到1.0000，同时保持较低的干净相对L2误差。干净标签、仅标签和打乱对照实验表明，高攻击成功率本身不足，成功的攻击必须在保持有界干净误差的同时将预测引导至预期的替代物理目标。

Conclusion: 研究结果揭示了一个结构性验证缺口：仅依赖平滑性或通用求解器般的行为不足以验证模型正确性，除非同时验证预期物理参数的来源。这为神经PDE算子的安全性验证提出了新的要求。

Abstract: Neural PDE operators are increasingly trained on reusable solver archives, yet validation often relies on clean prediction error and parameter-agnostic plausibility checks. We introduce cross-parameter relinking, a data-poisoning primitive that makes a triggered input select a valid solution from the same PDE family under an incorrect physical parameter. We term this a wrong-physics backdoor: the output remains physically plausible but is wrong for the intended parameter. The attack exploits tensor-to-parameter provenance failures in multi-parameter archives by stamping the surrogate input and relinking its supervision to a cached alternate-parameter solution for the same latent sample. Across 476 attack campaigns, we evaluate Burgers, advection-diffusion, two-dimensional Navier-Stokes, and an elliptic Poisson case. Fourier Neural Operators and DeepONet provide the primary evidence, with Transformer, GRU, and LSTM models as support. FNO reaches a backdoor success rate of 1.0000 on both advection-diffusion and two-dimensional Navier-Stokes while retaining low clean relative L2 error. Clean-label, label-only, and shuffled controls show that high attack success alone is insufficient: successful attacks must move predictions toward the intended alternate-physics target while preserving bounded clean error. These results expose a structural validation gap: smoothness or generic solver-like behavior is insufficient unless the provenance of the intended physical parameter is also verified.

</details>


### [10] [Decision Tree and K-Means Analysis of Raman Spectra for Edible Oils: A Physics-Informed AI Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20440)
*Amrita Shaw,Chandrasekar S. N.,Sai Muthukumar V.,Jhinuk Gupta,Deepak L. N. Kallepalli*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究建立了一个集成的拉曼光谱与机器学习框架，实现了食用油的精准鉴别。纯油样本分类准确率达100%，仅需4个光谱变量；含食品基质样本通过物理信息AI分解，分类准确率超过85%，同时大幅降低数据维度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 加工食品中食用油的鉴别对于食品质量、防欺诈和法规合规至关重要。现有方法面临食品基质干扰、光谱重叠等挑战，亟需一种高精度、可解释且低计算成本的检测方法。

Method: 采用拉曼光谱结合机器学习技术，包括t-SNE降维、K-means聚类、决策树分类和基于非负最小二乘法(NNLS)的物理信息AI光谱分解。对五种纯食用油和油炸薯片基质中的油进行检测，通过数据降维和光谱分解技术提取关键光谱特征。

Result: 纯油样本：决策树实现100%分类准确率，仅需4个拉曼变量(占原始1866个特征的0.21%)；含基质样本：经过纸张和马铃薯光谱扣除后，优化后的模型准确率分别达86.4%和85.4%，所需重要变量仅5个和4个。四特征表示将数据量减少99.44%且不损失分类精度。

Conclusion: 研究表明，通过物理意义明确、高度紧凑且可解释的光谱表征，可实现精准的拉曼油类识别。该方法为低成本AI、边缘AI、便携式传感和嵌入式食品质量监测提供了有前景的技术基础，具有重要的实际应用价值。

Abstract: Authentication of edible oils in processed foods is important for food quality, fraud prevention, and regulatory compliance. This study establishes an integrated Raman spectroscopy and machine-learning framework that links intrinsic spectral organization, interpretable classification, and Physics-Informed Artificial Intelligence (PI-AI). Five edible oils were investigated in pure form and within a fried-potato-chip matrix using t-SNE, K-means clustering, Decision Trees, and Non-Negative Least Squares (NNLS)-based spectral decomposition. Unsupervised analyses revealed substantially stronger class organization and separability in pure oils, whereas food-matrix effects introduced pronounced spectral overlap. Decision Trees achieved 100% classification accuracy for pure oils using only four Raman variables from the original 1866-feature spectral space. These four variables, consistently identified by both pre-pruned and post-pruned models, represented only approximately 0.21% of the available spectral information while retaining perfect test-set performance. For matrix-containing samples, NNLS-based PI-AI spectral decomposition substantially improved classification by separating oil-related signatures from paper and potato contributions. Optimized post-pruned models achieved accuracies of 86.4% and 85.4% for paper-subtracted and paper-plus-potato-subtracted datasets, respectively, while reducing the number of important Raman variables to only five and four. The compact four-feature representation further reduced the data footprint by 99.44% without loss of classification accuracy. Collectively, these findings demonstrate that accurate Raman-based oil identification can be achieved through physically meaningful, highly compact, and interpretable spectral representations, providing a promising foundation for Frugal AI, Edge AI, portable sensing, and embedded food-quality monitoring.

</details>


### [11] [BackDFL: A Unified Benchmark For Backdoor Attacks and Defenses In Decentralized Federated Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21137)
*Mouhamed Amine Bouchiha,Gregory Blanc,Yufei Han*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 去中心化联邦学习(DFL)对后门攻击的鲁棒性被高估；提出的BackDFL基准测试显示现有防御方法在恶意参与率低至15%时即失效，特别是在异构环境中。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有DFL安全研究存在重大缺陷：依赖简化的威胁模型、非自适应攻击者、碎片化的评估协议、不一致的通信拓扑和临时训练配置，导致对DFL安全性的理解不完整。去中心化架构移除了全局协调聚合，使DFL特别容易受到后门攻击。

Method: 提出BackDFL，一个统一的基准测试框架，用于在现实和自适应后门攻击条件下系统性地评估DFL。该框架考虑了更现实的威胁模型和自适应攻击策略。

Result: 通过大量实验，BackDFL揭示了去中心化学习的严重失效模式：最先进的拜占庭鲁棒DFL方法和适配的FL后门防御在中等恶意参与率（低至15%）下即失败，尤其在异构设置中；防御鲁棒性在不同通信图拓扑间差异显著。

Conclusion: DFL的鲁棒性被显著高估，现有防御方法在现实攻击条件下表现不佳，特别是在异构环境和不同的网络拓扑结构中，需要重新审视DFL的安全假设。

Abstract: Decentralized Federated Learning (DFL) promises trust-free collaborative learning by replacing the centralized parameter server with peer-to-peer model exchange. However, this architectural shift fundamentally reshapes the threat landscape. Without globally coordinated aggregation, DFL becomes particularly susceptible to backdoor attacks, in which malicious participants implant persistent hidden behaviors while maintaining high clean-task performance. In this paper, we argue that the robustness of DFL has been significantly overestimated. Existing studies rely on simplified threat models, non-adaptive adversaries, fragmented evaluation protocols, inconsistent communication topologies, and ad hoc training configurations, leading to an incomplete understanding of DFL security. To address these limitations, we present BackDFL, a unified benchmark for systematically evaluating DFL under realistic and adaptive backdoor attacks. Through extensive experiments, BackDFL exposes critical failure modes of decentralized learning. Our results demonstrate that both state-of-the-art Byzantine-robust DFL methods and adapted FL backdoor defenses fail under modest malicious participation rates (as low as 15%), especially in heterogeneous settings, while their robustness varies substantially across communication graph topologies.

</details>


### [12] [Shared Physics Responses Recover Hidden Rankings in Neural Operator Libraries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20441)
*Hanbing Liang,Fujun Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种无需真实参考解即可选择最优神经算子的框架，通过共享物理诊断实现了对模型排序的准确预测，并可用于改进预测结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在部署神经算子作为科学代理模型时，由于缺乏高保真参考解，难以评估和选择最优模型，这阻碍了科学计算中神经算子的可靠应用。

Method: 利用平方希尔伯特空间损失的性质，证明模型排序仅依赖于候选模型差异的低维张成空间，从而通过基于锚点的控制方程线性化响应这一单一共享物理诊断，同时对所有模型进行评分和排序。

Result: 在流体、反应-扩散和波动动力学的多种傅里叶和卷积算子库上，该方法准确恢复了超过99.6%的成对偏好和99.0%的最优检查点；修正后的物理代理通常优于最佳单个候选模型；并为强单调离散化建立了可计算且能严格保证精确决策的充分条件。

Conclusion: 该方法通过利用局部动力学响应而非原始缺陷幅度，实现了无需真实数据即可高效、可靠地部署科学代理模型，为神经算子在科学计算中的应用提供了有效的模型选择机制。

Abstract: Selecting the optimal neural-operator prediction during deployment is challenging when high-fidelity reference solutions are unavailable. We demonstrate that under a squared Hilbert-space loss, ranking a finite model library depends strictly on the low-dimensional span of candidate differences, allowing us to score all models simultaneously using a single anchor-based linearized response of the governing equation. This shared physical diagnostic accurately recovered over 99.6\% of pairwise preferences and 99.0\% of optimal checkpoints across diverse Fourier and convolutional operator libraries for fluid, reaction-diffusion, and wave dynamics. Furthermore, the corrected physical proxy frequently outperformed the best individual candidates, and we establish computable sufficient conditions that rigorously certify exact decisions for strongly monotone discretizations. By exploiting the local dynamical response rather than raw defect magnitude, this framework enables the reliable and highly efficient deployment of scientific surrogates without requiring ground-truth data.

</details>


### [13] [Thermo-FL: Thermal-Aware Robust Federated Fine-Tuning of Large Language Models for Edge AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21172)
*Shiva Shrestha,Kazi Shaharair Sharif,Zongxing Xie,Jiajing Huang,Anhao Xiang,Honghui Xu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了Thermo-FL框架，通过温度感知控制LoRA适配器训练和稀疏更新传输，同时解决了联邦学习中边缘设备的热约束和对抗性更新损坏问题，在保证模型性能的同时提高了系统的鲁棒性和稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 边缘设备上的联邦微调面临两大挑战：一是热约束设备可能因温度过高而节流、减慢本地训练或延迟同步聚合；二是拜占庭客户端和通信层攻击者可能破坏用于构建全局模型的更新。现有方法未能同时解决硬件不稳定性和对抗性攻击问题。

Method: Thermo-FL框架包含客户端和服务器端两部分：客户端根据设备温度动态调整LoRA层的激活比例和传输更新密度，在热应力下降低工作负载；服务器端引入TERRA鲁棒聚合管道，结合范数过滤、掩码感知方向验证、自适应活跃坐标裁剪和掩码感知聚合，处理动态稀疏LoRA更新。

Result: 在大规模模拟器中，Thermo-FL在对抗性稀疏聚合下提高了鲁棒性，在干净和攻击设置下均达到最强的BoolQ准确率，同时在GSM8K上保持竞争力。在Jetson物理测试平台上，Thermo-FL稳定了设备温度，通过位图稀疏编码减少了压缩上传大小，并在符号翻转/缩放和MITM扰动下保持GSM8K实用性。

Conclusion: 安全的边缘LLM适应应联合考虑硬件行为、工作负载调节、稀疏通信和聚合鲁棒性。Thermo-FL证明了温度感知控制与鲁棒聚合机制结合可以有效应对实际部署中的硬件约束和安全威胁。

Abstract: Federated fine-tuning enables large language models to adapt on edge devices without centralizing private data, but practical deployments must address hardware instability and adversarial update corruption together. Thermally constrained clients may throttle, slow local training, or delay synchronous aggregation, while Byzantine clients and communication-layer adversaries can corrupt the updates used to form the global model. To address these challenges, we present Thermo-FL, a thermal-aware federated LoRA fine-tuning framework that uses device temperature as an active control signal for local adapter training and sparse update transmission. On the client side, Thermo-FL adjusts the active LoRA-layer fraction and transmitted update density as devices heat or cool, reducing workload under thermal stress. On the server side, Thermo-FL introduces TERRA, a robust aggregation pipeline for dynamically sparse LoRA updates that combines norm filtering, mask-aware directional validation, adaptive active-coordinate clipping, and mask-aware aggregation. We evaluate Thermo-FL using both a large-scale emulator and a Jetson-based physical testbed. In the emulator, Thermo-FL improves robustness under adversarial sparse aggregation and achieves the strongest BoolQ accuracy across clean and attack settings while remaining competitive on GSM8K. In the physical prototype, Thermo-FL stabilizes device temperature, reduces compressed upload size through bitmap sparse encoding, and preserves GSM8K utility under sign-flip/scale and MITM perturbations. These results show that secure edge LLM adaptation should jointly consider hardware behavior, workload regulation, sparse communication, and aggregation robustness.

</details>


### [14] [Stored in Optimizer State, Valued by Later Training: A Causal Account of Subliminal Trait Transfer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20442)
*Qinyang Xu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究揭示了潜隐特质迁移的两阶段机制：优化器状态负责传输源扰动，后续训练决定其行为价值。通过状态手术发现，一阶矩是因果载体，仅移植一阶矩可在无源更新时产生参数差异，且能预测模型行为符号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究解释了潜隐信号如何进入梯度，但未阐明这些信号如何在源移除后存活，以及在后续训练中如何获得不同符号。研究旨在揭示特质信号在训练过程中的传播和存活机制。

Method: 将参数和优化器矩视为单一训练器状态，推导出精确的传输-估值恒等式，分离源扰动的观察者独立传播与未来延续和行为读出所赋的值。通过状态手术识别一阶矩作为因果载体，进行单因素移植实验验证。

Result: 实验表明：移植一阶矩单独保留参数、隐藏状态和输出在切割点不变，但无源更新会产生参数和隐藏状态差异；移植参数和一阶矩可恢复终端行为响应。相同源诱导差异通过匹配未来产生负、近零、正Qwen效应；完整horizon costate预测所有42个Qwen路径平均符号和所有21个Llama普通路径符号。

Conclusion: 确立了潜隐特质迁移的两阶段机制：优化器状态传输源扰动，后续训练决定其行为价值。观察者独立传输在多个模型架构（Qwen、SmolLM2、Llama）和非LoRA MNIST系统中复现，为理解和控制潜隐信号传播提供了理论基础。

Abstract: Subliminal trait transfer allows a student model to acquire behavioral dispositions from teacher-generated data in which the trait is not semantically expressed. Recent work explains how such signals enter gradients, but not how they survive source removal or acquire different signs under later training. We treat parameters and optimizer moments as a single trainer state and derive an exact transport-valuation identity separating observer-independent propagation of the source perturbation from the value assigned by a future continuation and behavioral readout. State surgery identifies the first moment as a causal carrier. Transplanting it alone leaves parameters, hidden states, and outputs unchanged at the cut, yet source-free updates generate growing parameter and hidden-state differences; transplanting parameters with the first moment recovers the terminal behavioral response. Sending the same source-induced difference through matched futures produces negative, near-zero, and positive Qwen effects (-0.658, +0.008, and +0.658 seed means). This ordering recurs in all 12 Llama-3.2-1B seeds after eight updates, while state-difference norms remain nearly equal across routes. Both contrasts grow in every paired seed when the continuation extends to sixteen updates. A full-horizon costate predicts all 42 Qwen route-mean signs and all 21 resolved Llama ordinary-route signs. Observer-independent transport also replicates across Qwen, SmolLM2, and Llama, while the complete-state recurrence predicts physical, hidden, and fixed-head responses in non-LoRA MNIST systems, including CNNs trained with AdamW and momentum SGD. Together, these results identify a two-stage mechanism for subliminal trait transfer: optimizer state transports the source perturbation, and later training determines its behavioral value.

</details>


### [15] [Amortized Bandwidth Learning for Kernel Density Estimation under Logarithmic Score](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20445)
*Junyi Liang,Hailiang Du*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种摊销框架来学习样本到带宽的映射关系，用于核密度估计中的带宽选择。该方法通过训练有限高斯混合模型，在多种密度估计任务上实现了稳定学习，并在小样本和异质样本情况下显著优于Silverman法则、Sheather-Jones选择器和最小二乘交叉验证等传统方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 核密度估计的性能关键依赖于带宽选择，而传统的带宽选择方法要么通过分析或渐近理论规定样本到带宽的规则，要么为每个样本求解新的优化问题。这些方法在不同任务间无法共享知识，且在处理异质任务时存在局限性。因此需要一种能够跨任务学习样本到带宽映射关系的框架。

Method: 提出摊销框架学习样本到带宽的映射：1）采用截断并重新归一化的有界支持公式实现跨异质任务的稳定学习；2）引入仿射标准化使在单个参考区间训练的选择器能够迁移到其他有界区间；3）使用有限高斯混合作为通用训练机制，利用其L¹逼近性质；4）通过对数评分进行优化。

Result: 在高斯采样、多族基准测试和随机高斯混合训练等实验中，摊销选择器一致且显著地优于Silverman法则、Sheather-Jones选择器和最小二乘交叉验证，在小样本和异质样本情况下优势尤为明显。训练后的选择器能够强泛化到不同的密度结构，可直接应用于来自未知密度的有限样本。

Conclusion: 该方法实现了广泛适用性与强实证性能的结合，使得同一训练好的选择器可以直接应用于来自未知密度的有限样本，无需指定或拟合分布族，为需要将有限样本或集成转换为连续概率密度的广泛应用提供了有吸引力的框架。

Abstract: Kernel density estimation converts finite samples into probability densities, but its performance depends critically on bandwidth selection. Classical selectors prescribe the sample-to-bandwidth rule analytically or asymptotically, or solve a new optimization for each sample. An amortized framework is proposed that instead learns this mapping across a distribution of density-estimation tasks by optimizing the logarithmic score. A truncated-and-renormalized bounded-support formulation enables stable learning across heterogeneous tasks, while affine standardization allows a selector trained on a single reference interval to transfer across bounded intervals. Experiments under Gaussian sampling, a multi-family benchmark, and randomized Gaussian-mixture training show that the amortized selector consistently and substantially outperforms Silverman's rule, the Sheather--Jones selector, and least-squares cross-validation, with especially large gains in small and heterogeneous samples. Finite Gaussian mixtures provide a generic training mechanism supported by their $L^1$ approximation property. Selectors trained in this way generalize strongly across different density structures, allowing the same trained selector to be applied directly to finite samples from unknown densities without specifying or fitting a distributional family. This combination of broad applicability and strong empirical performance makes the framework attractive for a wide range of applications in which finite samples or ensembles must be converted into continuous probability densities.

</details>


### [16] [Mutual information and sensitivity analysis for feature selection in customer targeting: a comparative study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20447)
*Nestor Barraza,Sergio Moro,Marcelo Ferreyra,Adolfo de la Peña*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文比较了互信息和基于数据的灵敏度分析两种特征选择技术在银行电话营销案例中的表现。研究发现互信息选择了13个特征，灵敏度分析选择了9个特征。两者各有优势：灵敏度分析在低假阳性率下表现更佳，而互信息在高假阳性率下略优。互信息仍是有效的特征选择方法，而灵敏度分析能以更少特征实现良好预测效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 特征选择是数据驱动知识发现项目中的关键任务。虽然已有多种技术用于识别对预测结果影响最大的特征，包括互信息和近年来出现的基于数据的灵敏度分析，但缺乏对这两种技术优缺点的系统性比较分析，需要实证研究来指导实际应用中的方法选择。

Method: 将互信息和基于数据的灵敏度分析两种特征选择技术应用于银行电话营销案例。通过两种技术分别识别对电话营销成功最具影响力的特征集（互信息筛选出13个特征，灵敏度分析筛选出9个特征），然后基于筛选后的特征集分别构建逻辑回归模型进行预测性能比较。

Result: 基于数据的灵敏度分析在较低假阳性率值下表现更好，而互信息在较高假阳性率下略胜一筹。如果银行管理者希望在不大规模损失成功案例的前提下略微降低联系成本，互信息是更好的选择。结果表明互信息虽然不是新方法，但仍是有效的特征选择方法；而基于数据的灵敏度分析能够以更少的特征实现良好的预测结果。

Conclusion: 两种特征选择技术各有优势，适用于不同的业务场景。互信息适合追求成本控制且不愿承担高成功损失风险的场景；基于数据的灵敏度分析则适合追求模型简洁性、用更少特征实现良好预测的场景。研究证实了互信息作为经典方法的持续有效性，同时也展示了新兴方法的优势。

Abstract: Feature selection is a highly relevant task in a data-driven knowledge discovery project. Several techniques have been developed aiming at finding the features that influence most an outcome to predict, including mutual information and, in recent years, the data-based sensitivity analysis. The present research focus on analyzing the advantages and disadvantages of each of these two techniques, by applying both to a bank telemarketing case. Thereafter, a logistic regression model is built on the tuned set of features identified by each of the two techniques as the most influencing set of features on the success of a telemarketing contact, in a total of 13 features for mutual information and 9 features for the data-based sensitivity analysis. The latter performs better for lower values of false positives while the former is slightly better for a higher false positive ratio. Thus, mutual information becomes a better choice if bank managers intend to reduce slightly the cost of contacts without risking losing a high number of successes. Such results show that mutual information, although not recent, is still a valid method for feature selection. On the other side, the data-based sensitivity analysis selection achieved good prediction results with less features.

</details>


### [17] [When Clean Data Hurts: Learning with Monotone Corruptions Beyond Binary Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20480)
*Julian Asilis,Shaddin Dughmi,Chirag Pabbaraju*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 单调对抗性攻击在多分类和部分二分类设置下，能使原本可学习的概念类变得不可学习；攻击者只需添加线性数量的恶意数据点即可实现攻击。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典PAC学习假设训练数据满足i.i.d.条件，但现实中存在数据被无关甚至对抗性源污染的情况。Larsen et al. (2026)提出了单调对抗性污染模型，发现所有已知的最优二分类学习器在这种设置下错误率会从O(d/n)增加到Ω(d log(n/d)/n)。Mehrotra (2026)证明了这个对数因子在二分类中是必要的，但多分类和部分二分类概念类的后果仍然未知。

Method: 论文从三个维度进行分析：(1) 构造DS维度仅为2的可学习多分类问题，证明其在单调对抗者攻击下变得完全不可学习，攻击者只需添加线性数量b=|S|=n的数据点；(2) 对部分二分类概念类展示类似结果；(3) 证明当自适应添加数量为o(n)时，所有概念类保持可学习性，且证明了该界限的紧密性；(4) 分析受限对抗者（已知常数预算、半自适应、健忘）下的可学习性。

Result: 主要结果包括：(1) 存在DS维度仅为2的多分类问题在单调对抗攻击下变得不可学习；(2) 部分二分类概念类存在类似结果；(3) 当自适应添加数量为o(n)时，所有概念类保持可学习，这个界限是紧密的；(4) 对受限对抗者（常数预算b=O(1)、半自适应者仅查看p比例数据、健忘者无法查看数据），经典多分类错误率O(d_DS/n)仍可实现。

Conclusion: 单调对抗者在多分类和部分二分类设置下比二分类设置具有更强的破坏力，能够使原本可学习的概念类变得不可学习。这种不可学习性在自适应添加o(n)数据点时被突破，表明攻击者的预算能力是关键因素。对受限对抗者的分析表明，合理限制攻击者的知识和能力可以保持学习器的性能。

Abstract: Optimal learners are tailored to exploit the i.i.d.\ data assumption underlying the classic PAC model. What if an i.i.d.\ training sample were corrupted with correctly labeled examples drawn from an otherwise unrelated, even adversarial source? This model of learning with monotone adversarial corruptions was recently introduced by Larsen et al. (2026), who demonstrated that all known optimal binary learners suffer increased error rates in this setting, from $O(d / n)$ in the PAC model to $Ω(d \log(n / d) / n)$ under monotone corruption. Mehrotra (2026) proved this logarithmic factor to be necessary for binary classification, but left open the consequences of corruption for more general learning settings, such as multiclass classification and partial binary concept classes.
  As our primary result, we demonstrate that monotone adversaries are frighteningly more powerful in each of these settings. We exhibit a learnable multiclass problem, of DS dimension only 2, that becomes altogether unlearnable under a monotone adversary, and show an analogous result for partial binary concept classes. These results are achieved by an adaptive adversary permitted to view the original i.i.d.\ training set $S$ and to insert $b < \infty$ corrupted datapoints into $S$. In the multiclass example, the adversary need only insert a linear number $b = |S| = n$ of datapoints.
  We complement these impossibility results by proving that every class remains learnable when the number of adaptive additions is $o(n)$, which our previous multiclass lower bound proves to be tight. We further observe that the classic multiclass error rate of $O(d_{\mathrm{DS}} / n)$ remains achievable against adaptive adversaries restricted to a known constant budget $b = O(1)$, against semi-adaptive adversaries viewing only a $p$-fraction of $S$ for $p \in (0, 1)$, and against oblivious adversaries that cannot view $S$.

</details>

*Anirudh Sundar,Min Chen,Divya Tadimeti,Gemma Zhang,Alice Li,Nigel Boachie Kumankumah,Pavan Uttej Ravva,Sadid Hasan,Somya Chatterjee,Pruthvi Prakash Navada,Xiao Wang,Yue Kang,Sulaiman Vesal,Larry Heck*

TL;DR: 本文提出了Metag数据集，用于加速元评审智能体的开发，该数据集通过记录审稿-回复过程中的论文修改，帮助元评审者快速识别作者是否解决了审稿意见以及修改位置。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI工具正在支持科研周期的各项任务，但会议投稿量的持续增长增加了元评审者的负担，他们需要综合审稿人反馈、作者回复和论文修订。为减轻这一负担并提高同行评审的透明度和可追溯性，需要开发能够自动识别论文修改的智能体。

Method: 通过获取审稿截止前和接收后的论文版本，计算两个文档之间的差异，并让人类标注者将这些差异与OpenReview讨论中的行动项进行对齐。每个实例包含审稿人关注点、作者的提议解决方案以及实施声明的论文差异。

Result: 构建了包含349个高质量行动项的Metag数据集，每个行动项都与论文修改相关联，数据集已在GitHub上公开。

Conclusion: 该数据集将支持开发使元评审者能够快速识别作者是否解决了审稿人意见以及修改位置的方法，从而提高同行评审过程的透明度和可追溯性。

Abstract: AI tools increasingly support tasks across the scientific research cycle, from experiment design and manuscript preparation to peer review. At the same time, the continuing growth in conference submissions has increased the burden on meta-reviewers, who must synthesize reviewer feedback, author rebuttals, and manuscript revisions. To address this concern, this paper introduces Metag, a dataset to accelerate the development of meta-reviewing agents, specifically to identify changes made to scientific articles during the review-rebuttal process. Each instance contains a reviewer concern, the author's proposed resolution, and the manuscript diffs implementing the stated change. Metag is collected by obtaining manuscript versions from before the review deadline and after acceptance, computing differences between the two documents, and asking human annotators to align these differences with action items from OpenReview discussions. The resulting dataset consists of 349 high-quality action items tied to paper differences and will enable building methods to empower meta reviewers to quickly identify whether authors have addressed reviewer statements and where in the paper those changes have been made, resulting in additional transparency and traceability throughout peer review. The dataset is publicly available at https://github.com/microsoft/Metag-dataset.

</details>


### [19] [When Graph-JEPA Learns the Wrong Thing: Diagnosing and Repairing Category-Conditional Collapse](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20516)
*Gollam Rabby,Sören Auer*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发现了联合嵌入预测架构（JEPA）评估指标的严重缺陷：线性探测准确率和有效秩看似健康，实际却无法检索任何实例信息。通过修复并引入新的评估框架，揭示了现有指标在无信息目标上的饱和问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有JEPA架构普遍采用线性探测和有效秩进行评估，但这些指标是否能真实反映表示质量缺乏验证。作者旨在揭示这些评估指标的潜在失效模式，确保评估框架的可靠性。

Method: 构建了包含57,903篇科学推理文章的图数据集，每篇文章作为一个子图。实现Graph-JEPA模型，预测子图中被遮蔽的方面。设计三组对照实验：零信息目标上的指标行为、方差分配分析、退化解的全局最优性验证。引入可归约性审计和目标质量门控机制。

Result: 发现指标失效现象：线性探测准确率0.871、有效秩18-47看似健康，但检索恢复率为0.00/14.4比特（MRR 1.9e-4与随机基线无显著差异，p=0.98）。追踪到方差分配异常：冻结输入将86.05%方差分配给子图身份、0.40%给方面身份，而训练后的潜在表示分别为0.39%和99.61%。证实退化解是耦合预测器/EMA目标的全局最小值。修复配置达到14.377/14.379比特，超过13.865比特的oracle基线。发现目标可归约：子图内边是节点普查的确定性函数。学习率调度的影响大于架构选择，比特数与推理探测无相关性。25.96%的节点是重复占位符。

Conclusion: 线性探测、有效秩和评估指标均可能在无信息或低质量目标上达到饱和，给出虚假的质量信号。需要引入可归约性审计和目标质量门控机制确保评估有效性。发布了包含这些机制的评估框架。研究强调了重新审视深度学习评估范式的必要性。

Abstract: Joint-embedding predictive architectures are selected almost universally by linear probing and effective rank. We report a case where both read healthily while the representation carries zero usable instance information. We repair it, and a second failure appears: the repaired metric saturates on a target carrying no structural information. Our corpus is a scientific-reasoning graph over 57,903 articles, each a subgraph. A Graph-JEPA predicts one masked aspect from a subgraph's remaining aspects, attaining linear-probe accuracy 0.871 and effective rank 18-47, yet retrieval recovers 0.00 of 14.4 bits (MRR 1.9e-4 vs chance 1.99e-4, p=0.98). Three upper bounds on the same pool and code recover nearly everything (+14.28, +14.34, +14.22 bits), ruling out corpus, masking, pool, and metric as causes. We trace this to variance allocation - frozen inputs place 86.05% of variance on subgraph identity and 0.40% on aspect identity, while trained latents place 0.39% and 99.61%. This is a property of the objective's optimum: the degenerate solution is a global minimum of the coupled predictor/EMA-target objective, present already at init. A repaired configuration reaches 14.377 of 14.379 bits, above the 13.865-bit oracle; reverting the loss to regression drops it to 0.307 bits, confirming it. Yet the repair licenses nothing about reasoning: the target is reducible, since intra-subgraph edges are a deterministic function of node census. The oracle reaches 96.4% of the ceiling, and our largest effect is the learning-rate schedule, not architecture. Bits and a reasoning probe show no relation across ten cells. A data-derived target fails a quality gate - 25.96% of nodes are duplicate placeholders, and the rest is more generic than supporting evidence. Rank, probes, and metrics can all saturate on an unsupportive evaluation. We release a harness with a reducibility audit and target gate.

</details>


### [20] [Learning Exact NVIDIA SASS Encoders with $\mathbb{F}_2$ Linear Algebra](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20532)
*Jiading Gai*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了F2Asm，这是首个能够学习NVIDIA SASS指令编码器的开源系统，支持Hopper、Blackwell和Rubin架构，在往返测试中实现了与原始二进制代码的精确匹配。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: NVIDIA为现代数据中心GPU提供了SASS反汇编器，但未公开相应的汇编器，这限制了可控的机器码重写和底层优化研究。

Method: F2Asm将SASS指令编码器建模为F2域上的向量值仿射映射，使用高斯消元法增量构建紧凑基并检测不一致性。系统将目标特定的控制位、重定位规则和CUBIN元数据与学习算法分离，利用3,225个CUBIN文件进行训练。

Result: 成功训练了支持Hopper SM90/SM90a、Blackwell SM100和Rubin SM107架构的编码器。在往返测试中，F2Asm重新汇编的SASS代码与原始CUBIN的可执行代码段完全匹配。

Conclusion: F2Asm是首个开源的NVIDIA SASS汇编器，通过学习F2域上的仿射映射实现了精确的指令编码，为GPU底层优化和机器码分析提供了新的工具。

Abstract: NVIDIA provides a SASS disassembler but no public SASS assembler for recent data-center GPUs, limiting controlled machine-code rewriting. We present F2Asm, which learns exact 128-bit SASS encoders from paired disassembly and original CUBIN instruction words. To our knowledge, F2Asm is the first system to learn SASS instruction encoders as vector-valued affine maps over F2 and the first open-source NVIDIA SASS assembler to support Rubin SM107. F2Asm uses Gaussian elimination over F2 to incrementally build a compact basis, detect inconsistencies, and reject inputs outside the learned span. F2Asm separates target-specific control bits, relocation rules, and CUBIN metadata from its learning algorithm. We train encoders for Hopper SM90/SM90a, Blackwell SM100, and Rubin SM107 using 3,225 CUBINs from pinned NVIDIA and third-party production libraries, CUDA 13.3 packages, and CUDA 13.4 Developer Preview archives. In round-trip tests, F2Asm reassembles the disassembled SASS for each CUBIN, and all compared executable text sections match the originals exactly.

</details>


### [21] [AgentDecarbonizer: Carbon-Aware Execution for AI Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20566)
*Leyi Yan,Shuangning Li,Sihang Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对AI代理工作流的高碳排放问题，提出了一种碳感知优化器AgentDecarbonizer，通过时间和空间调度策略，可减少高达57.9%的碳排放。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI代理通过长时间运行、目标导向的工作流执行复杂任务（如软件修复、数据分析），但其频繁的模型调用会产生大量碳排放。虽然截止期限的灵活性为碳感知执行提供了机会，但需要解决时间转移中的执行时间不确定性以及空间转移中缓存上下文重新计算的问题。

Method: 提出AgentDecarbonizer碳优化器，与OpenClaw代理框架并行运行。该方法根据任务提示和用户指定的截止期限，保守估计任务持续时间，选择符合截止期限的执行调度方案，同时在空间转移时考虑缓存重新计算的开销。系统利用WildClawBench基准进行评估，涵盖60个代理任务和四个电网区域。

Result: 在WildClawBench工作负载上评估表明，与碳无关的基线相比，AgentDecarbonizer减少了高达57.9%的碳排放；与在任务开始时选择碳最优电网的基线相比，减少了高达37.5%的碳排放。实验证明碳排放受令牌消耗、上下文缓存重用和电网碳强度影响。

Conclusion: AI代理工作流具有显著的碳减排潜力，通过利用截止期限灵活性进行时间和空间上的智能调度可以有效降低碳排放。AgentDecarbonizer成功解决了执行时间不确定性和缓存重新计算开销等关键挑战，为AI代理的可持续运行提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: AI agents extend large language models from single prompt-response interactions to long-running, goaldirected workflows that issue many model calls, invoke tools, and interact with external environments. These workflows enable tasks such as software repair, data analysis, and experiment management, but their repeated model invocations can incur substantial carbon emissions. This paper characterizes the carbon emissions of OpenClaw agent workloads using WildClawBench, and shows that emissions depend on token consumption, context cache reuse, and the carbon intensity of the grid. Our characterization identifies deadline flexibility as an opportunity for carbon-aware execution: agent tasks can wait for lower-carbon-intensity periods or shift to lower-carbon grids. However, doing so requires handling uncertain execution time for temporal shifting and cached context recomputation during spatial shifting. We present AgentDecarbonizer, a carbon optimizer for AI agents that runs alongside OpenClaw. Given a task prompt and user-specified deadline, AgentDecarbonizer conservatively estimates task duration and selects deadline-feasible execution schedules, while accounting for cache recomputation overhead during spatial shifting. Evaluated on WildClawBench workloads with 60 agent tasks across four grids, AgentDecarbonizer reduces carbon emissions by up to 57.9 % compared with a carbon-agnostic baseline and by up to 37.5 % compared with a baseline that selects the carbon-optimal grid at task start time.

</details>


### [22] [Provable Edge-of-Stability for Adam on a One-Dimensional Quadratic](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20638)
*Yiman Fong,Heng Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文在一维二次函数设置下研究了Adam优化器的边缘稳定性（EoS）现象，证明了Adam在广泛参数区域内表现出向其冻结稳定性阈值恢复的倾向，同时也发现了该边缘寻找机制失效的特定情况。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Adam优化器的边缘稳定性现象已被广泛观察到，但其背后的动力学机制尚未被充分理解。研究旨在通过一个简化的设置（一维二次函数）来分离出EoS背后的优化器诱导动力学，从而提供对该现象的动力学解释。

Method: 在常曲率的一维二次函数上分析未校正的Adam优化器，通过理论刻画不同参数空间下的动力学行为，证明Adam在广泛参数区域内向稳定性阈值恢复的倾向，并识别该机制失效的特定设置（包括严格亚临界周期轨道和特殊调节轨迹）。

Result: 证明了Adam在广泛参数区域内表现出向其冻结稳定性阈值$2(1+β_1)/[η(1-β_1)]$恢复的倾向；同时识别了边缘寻找机制失效的设置，包括严格亚临界周期轨道和保持均匀超临界但仍收敛到最优点的特殊调节轨迹。

Conclusion: 该研究在不涉及损失几何形状演变的设置中，为Adam的EoS现象提供了具体的动力学解释，同时揭示了该解释的局限性，为深入理解Adam优化器的动力学行为提供了理论框架。

Abstract: The edge-of-stability (EoS) phenomenon of Adam has been widely observed, while its underlying dynamical mechanism is not yet fully understood. We study uncorrected Adam on a one-dimensional quadratic, a clean setting where constant curvature isolates the optimizer-induced dynamics behind the EoS. We characterize the resulting dynamics across the parameter space. In broad regimes, we prove that Adam exhibits a restoring tendency toward its frozen stability threshold $2(1+β_1)/[η(1-β_1)]$. We also identify settings in which this edge-seeking mechanism breaks down, including strictly subcritical periodic orbits and specially tuned trajectories that converge to the optimum while remaining uniformly supercritical. These results give a concrete dynamical explanation for Adam's EoS in a setting free of evolving loss geometry, while also exposing its limitations.

</details>


### [23] [Meta-clustering of milk mid-infrared spectra identifies dairy cow groups associated with negative energy balance in early lactation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20653)
*T. Touil,E. R. Paquet*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究利用中红外光谱数据对早期泌乳奶牛进行聚类分析，识别出5个与能量平衡状态显著相关的奶牛群体，发现简单的PCA+k-means方法效果与复杂方法相当。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的聚类方法已用于识别牛奶样本、奶牛或牛群的群体，但直接对中红外(MIR)光谱数据应用聚类可以发现与牛奶性状或健康问题相关的潜在奶牛群体，有助于预防疾病或监控高风险动物。

Method: 基于407,632条单个牛奶MIR记录，结合光谱过滤、两种降维方法（PCA和自编码器）、两种聚类算法（k-means和谱聚类）共8种聚类方法，通过元聚类将相似聚类结果重新分组，最终识别出5个元聚类。

Result: 识别出5个显著关联牛奶性状的早期泌乳奶牛元聚类群体；8种方法收敛于相同的5个元聚类；经典高效的PCA结合k-means方法能复现复杂方法的聚类效果；5个元聚类与泌乳天数(DIM)强相关，反映了负能量平衡(NEB)严重程度的梯度。

Conclusion: 五个元聚类反映了NEB的不同严重程度：严重、中度、可能轻度NEB，以及两个恢复期群体（一个快速恢复，一个早期恢复）。简单的PCA+k-means方法在处理全谱数据时能达到与复杂方法相当的聚类效果，为实际应用提供了高效解决方案。

Abstract: Clustering methods have been used to identify distinct groups of milk samples, cows, or herds. Fourier-transform infrared (FTIR) spectroscopy, particularly mid-infrared (MIR) spectroscopy, has been applied to individual cow milk samples to predict various milk traits. Applying clustering directly to MIR spectral data may reveal latent groups of cows associated with milk traits or health disorders and can help prevent these conditions or monitor at-risk animals. This study aimed to identify groups of individual dairy cows in early lactation directly from milk MIR spectra and to analyze their associations with milk traits. Using a dataset of 407,632 individual milk MIR records from 3,408 commercial farms, we combined (i) spectral filtering that selects informative wavenumbers, (ii) two dimensionality-reduction methods: principal component analysis (PCA) and an autoencoder, and (iii) two clustering algorithms: k-means and spectral clustering to yield eight different clustering approaches. We regrouped the assigned clusters into meta-clusters that encompassed the most similar ones identified by the eight approaches. Our results revealed five distinct meta-clusters of early-lactation individual dairy cows significantly associated with milk traits. Despite substantial differences, the eight approaches converged on the same five meta-clusters, and the classic, computationally efficient PCA-based k-means approach using the full spectrum recaptured clusters identified by more sophisticated, computationally intensive approaches. The five meta-clusters were strongly associated with DIM and appeared to reflect a gradient of negative energy balance (NEB) severity: severe, moderate, and possibly mild, while the remaining two likely represented cows recovering from NEB, one with rapid restoration of energy balance and one in early recovery.

</details>


### [24] [RiskTraf: Risk-Extrapolated Residual Learning for Multi-Variate Traffic Flow Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20656)
*Guangyu Wang,Zhidan Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出了PEMSB-3V基准数据集和RiskTraf风险外推插件，解决了交通流预测中流量、速度、占用率三变量利用不充分及模型依赖状态特定捷径的问题，显著提升了预测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有交通流预测基准和模型未能可靠地利用流量、速度、占用率三个原始传感器测量值。现有数据集常忽略速度和占用率，或包含逻辑不一致记录。更重要的是，直接对三变量输入进行经验风险最小化可能利用依赖状态的捷径，因为流量、速度、占用率之间的关系在自由流和拥堵状态下显著不同。

Method: 提出PEMSB-3V基准数据集，保留PeMS检测器的流量、速度、占用率原始测量值。设计RiskTraf模型无关的风险外推残差插件：冻结已训练的时空骨干网络，从历史速度和占用率学习轻量级零起始残差头，构建有序交通风险环境，通过风险外推目标优化各时间步的流量修正，在不修改骨干网络的情况下缓解特定状态捷径相关性。

Result: 实验表明RiskTraf持续改进多种预测骨干网络，优于去偏和分布偏移适应方法，在保持骨干网络不变的情况下有效缓解了状态特定的捷径相关性问题。

Conclusion: 该工作为多变量交通传感器数据的可靠利用提供了标准基准和有效方法，通过风险外推策略避免了模型对状态特定捷径的依赖，为交通流预测领域提供了新的数据资源和模型增强方案。

Abstract: Traffic sensors commonly record flow, speed, and occupancy, but standard traffic flow forecasting benchmarks and models rarely exploit all three raw measurements reliably. Although speed and occupancy provide sensor-native traffic-state information beyond flow alone, existing releases often omit these variables, replace them with proxies, or contain logically inconsistent records. Moreover, direct empirical risk minimization over three-variable inputs may exploit regime-dependent shortcuts, as the relationships among flow, speed, and occupancy vary substantially between free-flow and congested states. We introduce \textbf{PEMSB-3V}, a public benchmark suite that preserves raw flow, speed, and occupancy measurements from PeMS detectors for flow prediction. We also propose \textbf{RiskTraf}, a model-agnostic risk-extrapolated residual plug-in. For each trained spatio-temporal backbone, RiskTraf freezes the selected checkpoint and learns a lightweight zero-start residual head from historical speed and occupancy. The residual head constructs ordered traffic-risk environments and optimizes horizon-wise flow corrections with a risk extrapolation objective, thereby mitigating regime-specific shortcut correlations without modifying the backbone. Extensive experiments demonstrate that RiskTraf consistently improves diverse forecasting backbones and outperforms debiasing and distribution-shift adaptation methods. Our code and benchmark are available at https://github.com/Guangyu4/RiskTraf.

</details>


### [25] [C-Score: Beyond Accuracy for Robustness Assessment in Semi-Supervised Learning under Open-World Unlabeled Contamination](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20667)
*Tsao-Lun Chen,Chi-Cheng Fu,Han-Yi E. Chou,Shun-Feng Su*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了开放世界下伪标签半监督学习（SSL）中的隐藏崩溃问题，提出C-Score评估框架，通过预测、特征表示和优化三个空间来诊断SSL在未标记数据被OOD样本污染时的内部退化，发现仅靠干净测试准确率无法检测这种隐藏退化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有伪标签SSL方法基于封闭世界假设，假设未标记数据与标记数据分布一致。但在实际部署中，未标记数据来自开放环境，可能包含OOD样本，这些样本会被错误地高置信度预测并作为有效训练样本，而干净测试准确率可能保持稳定，掩盖了模型内部的退化。

Method: 提出C-Score框架，从三个互补空间评估训练行为：(1)预测空间：PLE和CCI指标评估未标记预测行为；(2)特征表示空间：Sem-Drift评估相对于标记语义锚点的偏离；(3)优化空间：Grad-Align评估标记和未标记优化的兼容性。

Result: 在CIFAR-10/100上测试多种OOD源、不同污染比例和四种伪标签SSL算法。结果显示：SVHN污染下CCI上升超过280%，而最佳准确率与无污染基线相差不到3%；近OOD源（CIFAR-100、STL-10）导致高达14.9%的准确率崩溃（FlexMatch，r=0.5）。

Conclusion: 仅依靠干净准确率不足以评估开放世界环境中SSL的鲁棒性，需要内部诊断信号来更可靠地评估未标记污染下的鲁棒性。C-Score指标能够揭示干净准确率无法检测的隐藏退化。

Abstract: Pseudo-label-based semi-supervised learning has achieved strong performance due to its simplicity and scalability. However, it is typically developed under a closed-world assumption that unlabeled data are drawn from the same distribution as labeled data. In practical deployment, unlabeled data are often collected from open environments and may contain OOD samples. Under such contamination, OOD samples may still receive high-confidence predictions and be incorporated into training as if they were valid target examples. This creates an important evaluation problem: clean in-distribution test accuracy may appear stable even when the internal learning dynamics of SSL have already deteriorated. To address this issue, we study hidden collapse in pseudo-label-based SSL under open-world unlabeled contamination from a diagnostic evaluation perspective. We present C-Score, a compact framework that evaluates training behavior in three complementary spaces: prediction, feature representation, and optimization. C-Score includes PLE and CCI for unlabeled prediction behavior, Sem-Drift for deviation from labeled semantic anchors, and Grad-Align for the compatibility between labeled and unlabeled optimization. Experiments on CIFAR-10 and CIFAR-100 with multiple OOD sources, varying contamination ratios, and four pseudo-label-based SSL algorithms show that C-Score metrics reveal hidden degradation that clean accuracy alone fails to detect: under SVHN contamination, CCI rises over 280% while best-accuracy remains within 3% of the uncontaminated baseline; near-OOD sources (CIFAR-100, STL-10) cause up to 14.9% accuracy collapse (FlexMatch, r=0.5). The results suggest that clean accuracy alone is insufficient for evaluating SSL robustness in open-world environments, and that internal diagnostic signals are necessary for more reliable robustness assessment under unlabeled contamination.

</details>


### [26] [Lightweight Adaptive ReduNet via Hyperspherical Manifold Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20668)
*Zhenglin Huang,Qifa Yan,Bin Dai,Xiaohu Tang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了LA-ReduNet，一种轻量级自适应白盒神经网络架构，通过优化层间更新规则，在保持分类精度的同时，将所需层数和参数存储量大幅降低至原始ReduNet的约1/29。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 原始ReduNet采用最大编码率降低（MCR²）原则逐层构建网络，虽具数学可解释性，但往往需要大量层数才能使目标函数收敛，导致展开模块的参数存储开销过大。

Method: 提出LA-ReduNet架构，引入超球面流形学习与自适应步长，优化层间更新规则，使得判别性特征表示能在显著更少的展开层数内获得。

Result: 仿真实验表明，LA-ReduNet在保持相当分类精度的前提下，使MCR²目标达到稳定值所需的层数减少一个数量级，参数存储量仅需原始展开ReduNet模块的约1/29。

Conclusion: LA-ReduNet有效解决了ReduNet层数需求大、参数存储高的问题，为白盒神经网络提供了一种高效轻量的构建方案。

Abstract: In recent years, a white-box neural network called ReduNet has been proposed, which employs the maximal coding rate reduction (MCR$^2$) principle to transform raw data into low-dimensional discriminative features via a forward layer-wise construction process. Unlike traditional deep networks that rely on backpropagation, ReduNet explicitly derives the parameters of each layer from the features of its preceding layer, offering a mathematically interpretable paradigm. However, this layer-wise construction often requires a large number of layers for the MCR$^2$ objective to reach a stable value, which increases the parameter storage of the unfolded module. To address this issue, we propose LA-ReduNet, a lightweight adaptive architecture that refines the layer-wise update rule and enables discriminative feature representations to be obtained with substantially fewer unfolded layers. Specifically, LA-ReduNet employs hyperspherical manifold learning and adaptive step sizes, thereby reducing by an order of magnitude the number of layers required for the MCR$^2$ objective to reach a stable value. Simulation results demonstrate that, while maintaining comparable classification accuracy, LA-ReduNet requires significantly fewer layers for the MCR$^2$ objective to reach a stable value. Remarkably, under the considered experimental settings, LA-ReduNet requires only approximately $1/29$ of the parameter storage of the unfolded ReduNet module for the MCR$^2$ objective to reach a stable value.

</details>


### [27] [Reinforcement Learning for Continuous-Time Jump Markov Decision Processes with Applications to Network Dynamic Pricing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20680)
*Huiling Meng,Ningyuan Chen,Xuefeng Gao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文建立了连续时间跳跃马尔可夫决策过程（CTJMDPs）中强化学习的理论基础，提出了适用于一般离散状态空间的免模型q-学习算法，在网络动态定价实验中表现优于基准方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的连续时间强化学习方法（如受控扩散的q-学习）主要针对连续状态空间$\mathbb{R}^d$，依赖半鞅理论，无法直接应用于缺乏代数加减结构的一般离散状态空间的CTJMDPs。许多运筹学应用（如带资源约束的多产品动态定价）属于此类问题，因此需要填补这一理论空白。

Method: 作者研究CTJMDPs中的强化学习问题，涵盖一般离散状态空间和连续/离散动作空间。通过构建熵正则化的连续时间控制问题和随机策略来建模探索-利用权衡，建立了CTJMDPs的q-学习理论基础，并开发了免模型的q-学习算法。该方法避免了简单的离散化近似，直接在连续时间框架下处理问题。

Result: 在网络动态定价（Gallego and van Ryzin 1997模型）的数值实验中，所提出的强化学习算法能够可靠地学习到近最优策略，在解的质量和大规模网络实例的可扩展性方面持续优于标准基准方法。

Conclusion: 本文成功建立了CTJMDPs的q-学习理论框架，为具有一般离散状态空间的连续时间强化学习问题提供了有效的解决方案，在理论贡献和实证效果方面均展现出优势。

Abstract: We study reinforcement learning (RL) in Continuous-Time Jump Markov Decision Processes (CTJMDPs) featuring general discrete state spaces (which need not possess a vector space structure) and continuous/discrete action spaces. The setup covers many well-known applications in operations such as multi-product dynamic pricing with capacitated resources (Gallego and van Ryzin 1997). To model the exploration-exploitation tradeoff, we formulate an entropy-regularized continuous-time control problem with stochastic policies. Recent continuous-time RL techniques such as $q$-learning for controlled diffusions in (Jia and Zhou 2023) focus on continuous state spaces $\mathbb{R}^d$ and rely heavily on semimartingale theory in $\mathbb{R}^d$ for their theoretical analysis. Consequently, their methods cannot be directly applied to CTJMDPs with general discrete state spaces, which may lack the algebraic addition and subtraction structures inherent to Euclidean spaces. To bridge this gap, we establish the theoretical foundations of $q$-learning for CTJMDPs and develop model-free $q$-learning algorithms. Compared to naïve time discretization and approximating CTJMDPs using discrete-time MDPs, our approach has several conceptual and empirical benefits. Numerical experiments in network dynamic pricing (Gallego and van Ryzin 1997) show that our proposed RL algorithm reliably learns near-optimal policies and consistently outperforms standard benchmark methods, demonstrating superior solution quality and effective scalability to large-scale network instances.

</details>


### [28] [Geometric Regularization for Long-Tailed Semi-Supervised Learning via Gaussian Feature Bridges](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20710)
*Hongyang He,Xinyuan Song,Yan Zhong,Daizong Liu,Yanbin Li,Yang-fan He,Wenqiao Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了Gaussian Bridge Consistency (GBC)框架，通过构建高斯特征桥接路径解决长尾半监督学习中的噪声伪标签问题，显著提升了尾类性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实世界的半监督学习面临两个关键挑战：长尾标签分布和不准确的伪标签。这些问题会导致模型泛化能力下降，并加剧确认偏差（confirmation bias），使模型更倾向于预测头部类别而忽略尾类。

Method: 提出了GBC框架，包含三个核心组件：(1)动态Prototype Atlas存储各类别的标签和伪标签样本；(2)高斯特征桥在潜在空间构建从不确定预测到可靠类别原型的平滑路径，应用桥接一致性损失强制对齐；(3)BridgeMix策略，一种置信度感知的特征混合方法，通过插值样本和锚点对增强跨样本泛化能力。

Result: 在CIFAR10-LT和ImageNet-LT（USB基准）上的广泛实验验证了GBC在现实长尾半监督学习设置下的鲁棒性和有效性。该方法持续提升了尾类性能，同时保持了良好的可扩展性，且不牺牲整体性能。

Conclusion: GBC框架成功解决了长尾半监督学习中的关键挑战，通过语义插值路径和高斯特征桥接机制，有效缓解了噪声伪标签带来的确认偏差问题，为现实场景下的半监督学习提供了一个鲁棒且可扩展的解决方案。

Abstract: Real-world semi-supervised learning (SSL) often encounters significant challenges with long-tailed label distributions and noisy pseudo-labels, which hinder generalization and amplify confirmation bias. In this work, we introduce a novel framework, Gaussian Bridge Consistency (GBC), to address these challenges by constructing semantic interpolation paths between unlabeled samples and high-quality class anchors. Our method maintains a dynamic Prototype Atlas that stores a diverse and evolving set of labeled and pseudo-labeled exemplars per class. For each unlabeled instance, GBC forms a class-conditional Gaussian Feature Bridge in the latent space, enabling the student model to traverse a smooth trajectory from uncertain predictions to reliable class prototypes. A bridge consistency loss is applied along this path to enforce alignment with a geometrically interpolated target distribution. Furthermore, we propose BridgeMix, a confidence-aware feature mixing strategy that interpolates both sample and anchor pairs to amplify cross-sample generalization. Extensive experiments on CIFAR10-LT and ImageNet-LT (USB benchmarks) validate the robustness and effectiveness of GBC under realistic long-tailed SSL settings, consistently improving long tail-class performance without sacrificing scalability.

</details>


### [29] [Hidden Axis of Uncertainty: Latent-Posterior Alignment in Graph Neural Networks with Bayesian Output Layers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20758)
*Suk Hoon Choi,Damdae Park,Junhyuk Choi,Hyein Jung,Changsoo Kim,Ung Lee,Kyeongsu Kim*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发现图神经网络中贝叶斯输出层的预测不确定性降低并非源于后验收缩，而是源于一种新机制——潜在-后验对齐(LPA)，并据此提出了对齐引导学习(AGL)方法，有效降低了预测不确定性并改善了结构校准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 贝叶斯神经网络的预测不确定性量化机制尚不清晰。传统理论将不确定性降低归因于后验收缩，但该假设对深度模型未必成立。本文旨在探究图神经网络中贝叶斯输出层的预测不确定性究竟如何形成，并揭示塑造不确定性的真正机制。

Method: 通过对具有贝叶斯输出层的图神经网络进行观察和干预实验，发现预测不确定性随着潜在表示向低方差后验方向移动而降低，但后验方差并未收缩。将此现象命名为潜在-后验对齐(LPA)。基于此发现，提出对齐引导学习(AGL)方法，在训练过程中显式促进这种对齐。

Result: AGL方法在保持预测准确率的同时有效降低了预测不确定性。更重要的是，AGL改善了结构校准，使模型置信度能够真实反映底层数据密度。干预实验验证了LPA在塑造预测不确定性方面的功能性作用。

Conclusion: 本研究为理解具有平均场贝叶斯输出层的图神经网络中的不确定性动态提供了新视角，将关注点从后验幅度转向潜在空间与参数空间之间的几何相互作用。这一发现挑战了传统的后验收缩理论，为贝叶斯深度学习中的不确定性量化提供了新的理论框架和实践指导。

Abstract: Bayesian Neural Networks (BNNs) with Bayesian output layers provide a principled and tractable framework for quantifying predictive uncertainty, yet the mechanisms shaping that uncertainty remain unclear. While conventional theory attributes uncertainty reduction to posterior contraction, the corresponding assumptions need not hold for deep models. In the Graph Neural Networks (GNNs) with Bayesian output layers studied here, we observe that predictive uncertainty decreases as latent representations shift toward lower-variance posterior directions, even though the posterior variance does not contract. We term this behavior Latent-Posterior Alignment (LPA) and conduct interventional experiments that support its functional role in shaping predictive uncertainty. Building on this insight, we propose Alignment-Guided Learning (AGL), which explicitly promotes this alignment during training. AGL effectively reduces predictive uncertainty while preserving accuracy and improves structural calibration, ensuring that the model confidence faithfully mirrors underlying data density. These findings provide a new perspective on uncertainty dynamics in GNNs with mean-field Bayesian output layers, shifting the focus from the magnitude of the posterior to the geometric interplay between latent and parameter spaces.

</details>


### [30] [Fuzzy-MoE: Interpretable Regime-Conditioned Expert Routing for Non-Stationary Multivariate Time Series Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20761)
*Lan Guo,Jie Xiao,Zhao Su,Jun Shen,Haoran Li,Weixia Ma,Qingguo Zhou,Binbin Yong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Fuzzy-MoE模型，通过模糊逻辑实现可解释的专家路由，有效捕捉非平稳多变量时间序列中的异质动态，显著提升预测精度和可解释性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非平稳多变量时间序列中，不同变量和样本表现出异质的潜在动态状态，而现有深度预测模型将其压缩为统一的端到端映射，导致对时变动态建模效果不佳，且缺乏在不同潜在状态下激活何种预测机制的可解释性。

Method: 将时间序列预测重构为潜在时间状态识别和可解释专家路由的统一框架。Fuzzy-MoE包含多个并行专家映射网络和双视图模糊路由器，路由器通过联合利用局部卷积动态和全局分段统计推断潜在时间状态，并通过可学习的Gaussian隶属函数计算专家激活强度，实现显式的IF-THEN规则专家选择，允许同一序列中的不同变量激活不同专家。

Result: 在多个公共时间序列基准数据集上，Fuzzy-MoE显著优于主流预测方法的预测精度。模糊隶属度和规则激活提供可解释的路由诊断，验证了框架在预测性能和机制透明度方面的有效性。

Conclusion: Fuzzy-MoE通过基于清晰可解释模糊规则的路由，实现了专家选择的透明性和可追溯性，解决了传统MoE模型黑盒路由的问题，为时间序列预测提供了一个兼具高性能和高可解释性的解决方案。

Abstract: In non-stationary multivariate time series, different variables and samples often exhibit heterogeneous latent dynamic states, while existing deep forecasting models usually compress them into a unified end-to-end mapping, leading to suboptimal modeling of time-varying dynamics and limited interpretability regarding which forecasting mechanism is activated under different latent states. To overcome these limitations, we reformulate time series forecasting as a unified framework of latent temporal state identification and interpretable expert routing, and propose Fuzzy-MoE, a fuzzy logic-based dynamic Mixture-of-Experts model. Fuzzy-MoE consists of multiple parallel expert mapping networks and a dual-view fuzzy router. By jointly exploiting local convolutional dynamics and global segmented statistics, the router infers latent temporal states and computes expert activation strengths through learnable Gaussian membership functions, enabling explicit IF-THEN rule-based expert selection. This fine-grained routing strategy allows different variables within the same sequence to activate different experts, effectively capturing heterogeneous temporal dynamics while improving model interpretability. Experimental results on multiple public time series benchmark datasets show that Fuzzy-MoE significantly outperforms mainstream forecasting methods in forecasting accuracy. Moreover, fuzzy memberships and rule activations provide interpretable routing diagnostics, demonstrating the effectiveness of the proposed framework in both forecasting performance and mechanism transparency. Unlike traditional MoE models that use black-box routing, Fuzzy-MoE`s routing is based on clear, interpretable fuzzy rules. This makes the expert selection transparent and traceable.

</details>
