The same token-level presence gives local O-components (OFFN, ONorm, and OInject), presence-weighted OStandardize, the O-Closure law \(M(H\oplus0)=M(H)\oplus0\), and an OTransformer by residual and compositional closure.
  The canonical operator is checked by contract tests and a GPU evaluation. In a zero-fine-tuning retrofit of a cloned pretrained TabPFN v3 regressor, calibrated hidden-carrier OAttention and Full-O variants change mean RMSE by $+0.088\%$ and $+0.177\%$, respectively, over 18 matched dataset--seed cases. A two-block ablation shows that OAttention alone does not preserve a NULL state through ordinary host components, whereas the OTransformer path does. These are scoped tests of exactness, active-path compatibility, and compositional necessity; they do not establish universal no-loss, arbitrary-host closure, learned attraction to the origin, or a general semantics for missing values.

</details>


### [135] [SENTRY: Deterministic, Intelligent Risk Assessment for IT Change Management](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21203)
*Daniel Arulpragasam,Christer Henrysson,Ella Ly,Deepika Anbalagan,Leo Feng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: SENTRY是一个基于机器学习的技术变更风险评估平台，通过XGBoost和混合RAG技术替代传统问卷调查，在大型金融机构中实现了0.87的ROC AUC和85%的准确率，高风险变更检测率提升约3.25倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型金融机构的技术变更管理依赖于准确、一致且可审计的风险评估，但现有实践仍依赖主观性强、易被操纵且难以区分常规变更与高风险变更的问卷调查方法。

Method: 提出SENTRY平台，采用基于XGBoost和混合检索增强生成（RAG）的确定性机器学习流程。系统整合结构化运营元数据、应用依赖图和历史事故记录，通过混合语义和词汇搜索检索历史变更请求，将非结构化变更请求文本的风险信号压缩为单一标量特征，并通过SHAP值保持模型可解释性。

Result: 在企业级变更数据上评估，SENTRY实现了0.87的ROC AUC和85%的整体准确率，高风险变更检测率约为现有流程的3.25倍。

Conclusion: 探讨了该设计背后的架构权衡及其对受监管变更管理中机器学习应用的启示，证明了在保持可解释性和确定性的前提下，机器学习方法可以显著提升风险评估性能。

Abstract: Technology change management in large financial institutions depends on risk assessments that are accurate, consistent, and auditable. In practice, many institutions still rely on self-reported questionnaires. Those questionnaires are subjective, easy to game, and poor at separating routine changes from the ones that later trigger major incidents. This paper presents SENTRY, a risk assessment platform that replaces questionnaire-based scoring with a deterministic machine learning pipeline built from gradient-boosted decision trees (XGBoost) and hybrid retrieval-augmented generation (RAG). The system combines structured operational metadata, application dependency graphs, and historical incident records with a hybrid semantic and lexical search over historical change requests. The retrieval step captures the risk signal in unstructured change request text, then compresses that signal into a single scalar feature before model inference. That design keeps the model deterministic and preserves per-prediction explainability via SHAP values. Evaluated on enterprise-scale change data, SENTRY achieves a ROC AUC of 0.87 and 85% overall accuracy, and it detects high-risk changes at roughly 3.25 times the rate of the existing process. We close by examining the architectural trade-offs behind this design and what they imply for the use of machine learning in regulated change management.

</details>


### [136] [Enhancing LLMs in Predictive Political QA with Semi-Structured Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21218)
*Yinan Liu,Zihan Zhou,Zichun Jin,Xinyu Wang,Bin Wang,Xiaochun Yang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出PSL双视图框架，通过提取政治行动者的立场信号和结构信号来增强LLM在预测性政治问答任务中的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的LLM增强方法（包括行动者画像模拟和知识图谱证据注入）在政治推理中主要将外部资源视为基于知识的证据，忽略了对预测有重要价值的信号：行动者在特定议题上的立场偏好以及政治行动者之间的间接依赖关系。

Method: 提出PSL双视图框架：语义视图从问题相关的行动者记录中提取立场信号；向量视图从行动者交互图中学习结构感知的行动者表示。该框架将半结构化政治记录转换为面向推理的证据。

Result: 在三个真实世界数据集和多个LLM上的实验表明，PSL持续优于基线方法，消融实验证实了立场信号和结构信号带来互补性增益。

Conclusion: 立场信号和结构信号对于预测性政治问答任务具有重要价值，双视图框架能有效整合这两种互补信号以提升LLM的政治预测能力。

Abstract: Predictive political question answering (QA), such as predicting how a political actor will vote, goes beyond factual lookup. External political resources offer rich historical evidence, but rarely contain the answer itself. Existing LLM augmentation methods, including actor-profile-based simulation and knowledge graph evidence injection, improve political reasoning but largely treat external resources as knowledge-based evidence, leaving prediction-relevant signals under-modeled. We identify two complementary signals for predictive political QA: actor stances that capture issue-specific preferences, and high-order structure signals that capture indirect dependencies among political actors. We propose PSL, a dual-view framework that converts semi-structured political records into inference-oriented evidence for LLMs. PSL extracts stance signals from question-relevant actor records in a semantic view, and learns structure-aware actor representations from an actor interaction graph in a vector view. Across three real-world datasets and multiple LLMs, PSL consistently outperforms baselines, with ablations confirming the complementary gains of stance and structure signals.

</details>


### [137] [Ontology-supported AI Model and Dataset Management](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21224)
*Jan Novacek,Ali Ahari,Tobias Müller,Sebastian Reiter,Alexander Viehl,Oliver Bringmann*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个用于工业环境下AI模型及相关资产有效交换与管理的平台，通过集成本体论来解决语义鸿沟问题，促进AI模型和数据集的使用、交换与分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着AI方法研究的快速发展，不同组织生成并使用大量AI资产（模型、数据集等），这些资产需要被跟踪、追溯和管理。同时，发现与当前任务相关的资产也至关重要。然而，在工业背景下有效交换和管理AI模型及相关资产时存在语义鸿沟问题，亟需解决。

Method: 作者开发了一个AI模型交换平台，该平台集成了一个本体论（ontology），能够促进对AI模型交换和管理任务所需内容的深入共同理解。平台支持AI模型和数据集的使用、交换和分析功能。

Result: 通过一个实时关键系统环境下的实际用例演示了平台的实用性，验证了平台在AI资产交换和管理方面的有效性。

Conclusion: 所提出的平台与本体论能够有效解决工业环境中AI模型及相关资产交换管理中的语义鸿沟问题，为AI资产的透明比较、进度跟踪和深入理解提供了支持。

Abstract: Recently, there has been a great deal of research into improving AI methods and their application. The main focus is on tracking progress, enabling transparent comparisons, and fostering a more profound understanding of AI. In that process, different organizations generate and use plenty of assets that need to be tracked, traced and managed. Moreover, it is important to discover assets relevant for the task at hand. This paper presents research aiming to contribute to answering the question of what is required to exchange and manage AI models and related assets effectively without semantic gaps in an industrial context. We introduce a platform for AI model exchange, which facilitates the usage, exchange, and analysis of AI models and datasets. The platform incorporates an ontology that can foster a more profound common understanding of what is required in these tasks and help tackle the issues mentioned above. Finally, we elucidate the utility of the platform through the illustration of a use case in the context of real-time critical systems.

</details>


### [138] [Fine-Grain GPU Parallelization of the Generalized Partition Crossover for Large-Scale Traveling Salesman Problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21233)
*Swetha Varadarajan,Darrell Whitley*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了针对大规模TSP问题的广义分区交叉(GPX)算子在GPU上的细粒度并行实现，通过将GPX分区重构为图并行问题，实现了48x-625x的加速比，显著提升了遗传算法求解器的可扩展性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于遗传算法(如EAX)的TSP求解器虽然在基准测试中表现优异，但在大规模并行架构上的可扩展性受限。主要瓶颈在于交叉操作涉及不规则的内存访问模式、图遍历和顺序依赖性，而现有GPU求解器仅利用种群级并行性，受限于较小的问题规模。

Method: 将GPX算子的分区阶段重构为图并行问题，采用合并内存布局、幽灵节点变换和连通分量分析技术。使用CUDA并行化父代路径的并集、度四顶点分裂、公共边删除以及重组组件识别等操作，实现了算子级别的细粒度并行化。

Result: 在10,000到200万城市的测试实例上，相比朴素顺序CPU实现获得了48x到625x的加速比，同时显著降低了内存开销。实验验证了算子级并行化能有效提升GA-based TSP求解器在现代众核架构上的可扩展性。

Conclusion: 通过创新的图并行重构方法，成功实现了交叉算子的细粒度GPU并行化，突破了传统种群级并行化的限制，为大规模TSP问题的高效求解提供了新的技术路径。

Abstract: The Traveling Salesman Problem (TSP) is one of the most extensively studied NP-hard optimization problems. Genetic Algorithm (GA)-based solvers, such as the Edge Assembly Crossover (EAX), achieve state-of-the-art performance on many benchmark instances. However, the scalability of these approaches in massively parallel architectures remains limited because crossover operations involve irregular memory access patterns, graph traversals, and sequential dependencies. Existing GPU-based TSP solvers primarily exploit population-level parallelism and are limited to relatively small problem sizes. This work presents a fine-grain GPU implementation of the partition phase of the Generalized Partition Crossover (GPX) operator for large-scale TSP instances. The proposed approach reformulates GPX partitioning as a graph-parallel problem using coalesced memory layouts, ghost-node transformations, and connected-component analysis. The im- plementation parallelizes the union of parent tours, the splitting of degree- four vertices, the deletion of common edges, and the identification of recombining components using CUDA. Experimental results on instances ranging from 10,000 to 2 million cities demonstrate substantial acceleration over a naive sequential CPU imple- mentation. The proposed GPU partitioning achieves speedups between 48x and 625x while significantly reducing memory overhead. The re- sults demonstrate that operator-level parallelism can substantially im- prove the scalability of GA-based TSP solvers on modern many-core architectures.

</details>


### [139] [CLEAR: Continuous Latent Adapter Routing for Utility-Preserving LLM Safety Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21278)
*Chengxiao Wang,Enyi Jiang,Xiaojing Liao,Sanmi Koyejo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出CLEAR框架，通过轻量级隐藏状态门控机制连续控制安全低秩适配器的激活强度，在显著降低有害输出的同时保留了模型在良性任务上的性能，有效改善了大语言模型安全性与实用性之间的权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的大语言模型安全调优方法（如全局应用的SFT或LoRA）虽然能提升安全性，但往往以牺牲实用性为代价，因为这些全局性的安全调优会同时影响模型对有害和良性输入的响应。如何在提升安全性的同时避免不必要的性能下降成为亟待解决的问题。

Method: 提出连续潜在适配器路由（CLEAR）框架，这是一个条件性安全适应方法。该方法使用轻量级的隐藏状态门控机制来连续控制安全低秩适配器（safety LoRA）的激活强度，从而实现针对不同输入动态调整安全干预的程度，避免对冻结的主干模型进行不必要的全局修改。

Result: 在Llama-3-8B-Instruct上的实验显示：CLEAR将HarmBench的攻击成功率（ASR）从32.3%降至0.5%，同时在GSM8K上的准确率比全局SFT或LoRA高出7.1个百分点，成功保留了基础模型的大部分实用性。在广泛使用的安全性和实用性基准测试中，CLEAR展现出比全局安全调优更强的鲁棒性和更低的实用性损失。

Conclusion: CLEAR为改善大语言模型对齐中的安全性-实用性权衡提供了一种有前景的机制，证明了通过条件性、自适应的安全适配可以在显著提升模型安全性的同时最小化对良性任务性能的影响，为LLM安全对齐研究提供了新的技术路径。

Abstract: Improving the safety of large language models (LLMs) often comes at the expense of utility, as globally applied safety tuning may affect model responses to both harmful and benign inputs. We propose \textbf{C}ontinuous \textbf{L}at\textbf{E}nt \textbf{A}dapter \textbf{R}outing (CLEAR), a conditional safety adaptation framework that uses a lightweight hidden-state gate to continuously control the activation strength of a safety low-rank adapter. CLEAR aims to reduce harmful completions while avoiding unnecessary changes to the frozen backbone that could degrade performance on benign prompts. Experiments on widely used safety and utility benchmarks show that CLEAR improves robustness on HarmBench while reducing the utility degradation observed with globally applied safety tuning such as SFT or standard low-rank adaptation (LoRA). On Llama-3-8B-Instruct, CLEAR reduces HarmBench ASR from 32.3\% to 0.5\%, while retaining most of the base model's utility and achieving up to 7.1 percentage points higher GSM8K accuracy than globally applied SFT or LoRA. These results suggest that CLEAR is a promising mechanism for improving the safety--utility trade-off in LLM alignment.

</details>


### [140] [AUSO: Action-Level Unified Skill Optimization from Internalization to Utilization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21292)
*Huizu Lin,Chengkai Huang,Tianqi Gao,Tao Huang,Daijiao Liu,Tongxin Li,Xiaoyan Sun,Lina Yao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出AUSO框架，通过渐进式、动作感知的优化过程统一技能学习与使用，使技能在训练过程中逐步从外部监督源转变为根据动作级效益自适应利用的决策知识。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法未能建模技能的完整生命周期（先提供可学习知识，再支持能力形成，最后仅在改善决策时调用），要么将技能保持在外部、要么完全内部化，或通过噪声任务级成功率在内部化与利用目标间选择，导致训练碎片化且无法区分同一轨迹中技能引导对不同动作的差异性影响。

Method: AUSO采用三阶段渐进式优化：训练初期联合从教师指导和环境结果中学习，获取基础技能同时保持任务导向反馈；随后强调基于结果的策略优化以巩固自主问题解决能力；策略成熟后，在技能条件和技能无关两种上下文中评估每个采样动作，将动作级信息信号与轨迹结果优势结合，使有益的技能敏感动作获得更强更新、有害动作被抑制。

Result: 在ALFWorld、WebShop和SearchQA三个基准上的实验表明，AUSO相比竞争基线方法在代理性能和分布外泛化能力上均有持续提升。

Conclusion: AUSO成功统一了技能学习与技能使用，实现了技能从外部监督到决策知识的渐进转变，其利用率根据动作级效益自适应调整，为强化学习代理的技能管理提供了新的有效范式。

Abstract: Skills play different roles as an agent's policy evolves: they should first provide learnable knowledge, then support capability formation, and finally be invoked only when they improve individual decisions. Existing methods rarely model this lifecycle. They either keep skills outside the model, fully internalize them, or select among internalization and utilization objectives through noisy task-level success rates. Such designs fragment training and assign uniform importance to actions within the same trajectory, even though skill guidance may help some decisions while distracting others. To solve these problems, we introduce AUSO (Action-level Unified Skill Optimization), which unifies skill learning and skill use through a progressive, action-aware optimization process. At the beginning of training, AUSO jointly learns from teacher guidance and environmental outcomes, enabling the policy to acquire foundational skills without losing task-oriented feedback. It subsequently emphasizes outcome-based policy optimization to consolidate autonomous problem-solving ability. As the policy matures, AUSO evaluates each sampled action under both skill-conditioned and skill-free contexts. The resulting action-level information signal is coupled with the trajectory outcome advantage, allowing beneficial skill-sensitive actions to receive stronger updates and harmful ones to be suppressed. Therefore, skills gradually transition from an external source of supervision into decision knowledge whose utilization is adapted to its action-level benefit, while reinforcement learning remains the shared backbone across all stages. Experiments on ALFWorld, WebShop, and SearchQA show that AUSO consistently improves agent performance and out-of-distribution generalization over competitive baselines.

</details>


### [141] [From Regulation to Implementation: A Critical Evaluation of LLM-Assisted Regulatory Compliance in Industry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21317)
*Adriana Watson,Marco Bücheler,Grant Richards*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究探讨了数据提取指令和监管模糊性对LLM生成的合规文档（如数字产品护照DPP和数据保护影响评估DPIA）质量与一致性的影响，发现宽松指南需要更高上下文提示，而严格指南虽能保证一致性但可能导致更多幻觉。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 欧盟在可持续性和隐私法规方面处于领先地位，推出了数字产品护照(DPP)和数据保护影响评估(DPIA)等合规文档要求。然而，创建这些合规文档面临挑战：工业数据格式异构且分散，难以提取为合规的DPP格式；DPIA文档需要跨学科专业知识且缺乏标准化格式。尽管研究者提出使用LLM辅助生成，但这些问题对LLM输出质量的影响尚未得到充分研究。

Method: 通过对比不同严格程度的合规指南（严格的数字电池护照DBP格式与宽松的DPIA格式），测试不同模型在不同上下文提示条件下的表现。研究人员手动创建基准真值模式，将LLM生成的合规文档与真值进行对比评估，分析数据提取指令和监管模糊性对输出质量的影响。

Result: 研究发现：对于较宽松的指南（如DPIA格式），需要更高上下文的提示才能保持一致性和完整性；而更严格的指南（如数字电池护照DBP格式）无论提示上下文如何都能产生一致结果，但可能导致输出中出现更多幻觉现象。

Conclusion: 监管指南的严格程度显著影响LLM生成合规文档的质量特征。宽松指南需要更精心的提示工程来确保质量，而严格指南虽然能保证一致性但需要关注幻觉问题。这为实践中使用LLM生成合规文档提供了重要指导。

Abstract: The European Union (EU) has emerged as a leading regulatory body in the development of sustainability and privacy regulations. While new regulation requirements vary, many include a documentation artifact to ensure compliance. Notably, the Ecodesign for Sustainable Products Regulation (ESPR) introduces Digital Product Passports (DPPs) for life cycle transparency, while the General Data Protection Regulation (GDPR) mandates Data Protection Impact Assessments (DPIAs) to mitigate privacy risks. Creating these compliance artifacts, however, is challenging. Industrial data, which often exists in heterogeneous formats and is scattered across company and supplier systems, is required for DPPs and can be difficult to extract into compliant DPP formatting. Furthermore, DPIA documents require interdisciplinary expertise and follow no standardized format, making development difficult for novel systems. To address the particular complexity of compliance artifact creation for both regulations, researchers have proposed the use of LLMs in the generation process; however, the impact of the aforementioned problems on the output of these systems is largely unaddressed. This work investigates the existing research gap by exploring how data extraction instructions and regulatory vagueness impact the quality and consistency of LLM-produced compliance artifacts. The resulting artifacts are evaluated by benchmarking different models against manually created ground-truth schemas. The results reveal that less strict guidelines, such as DPIA formatting, require higher context prompts to maintain consistency and completeness. Stricter guidelines, such as formatting for Digital Battery Passports (DBP), result in consistent results regardless of prompt context, but may lead to more hallucinations in the output

</details>


### [142] [Unified Branch-and-Bound Search for the Steiner Traveling Salesman Problem on Graphs of Convex Sets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21319)
*Jingtao Tang,Hang Ma*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文形式化了凸集图上的Steiner旅行商问题，提出了基于分支定界的统一搜索方法，通过根节点行走前缀枚举和连通流松弛下界，实现了对无限解空间的有效搜索，并在移动机械臂检测任务中验证了方法的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的Steiner旅行商问题在凸集图上具有无限的解空间（包括可选中转顶点和允许重复访问），现有方法难以有效处理这种复杂性，因此需要开发能够在无限解空间中寻找最优或近最优解的算法框架。

Method: 提出了统一的分支定界搜索框架，枚举根节点行走前缀。使用加性下界图成本约束已提交前缀，并通过割分离的连通流松弛来下界访问剩余目标并返回根节点的剩余成本。实现了最佳优先和深度优先两种遍历策略，并在均匀正成本假设下证明了算法的有限终止性。

Result: 两种遍历策略在所有基准实例上均能在30秒内找到可行解，平均认证最优性间隙分别为28.1%和29.7%。相比之下，两个最近的基线方法只能在大约一半的实例上成功求解。方法成功应用于包含LTLf动作先后约束的移动机械臂检测任务，实现了感知模式、访问顺序和连续轨迹的联合选择。

Conclusion: 该方法为凸集图上的Steiner旅行商问题提供了首个有效的全局最优性保证算法，能够处理无限解空间，并成功应用于实际的移动机械臂检测任务规划问题，显著优于现有方法。

Abstract: We formalize the Steiner Traveling Salesman Problem (Steiner-TSP) on Graphs of Convex Sets (GCS), which seeks a minimum-cost closed trajectory through required convex sets while allowing optional transit vertices and revisits. To explore the resulting infinite solution space, we propose a unified branch-and-bound search over rooted walk prefixes. Additive lower-bound-graph costs bound committed prefixes, while a cut-separated connected-flow relaxation lower-bounds the residual cost of visiting every remaining target and returning to the root. Under a uniform positive-cost assumption, best-first traversal terminates after finitely many expansions on every feasible instance without an initial incumbent, whereas depth-first traversal does so once a finite incumbent is available. For a user-specified factor $ε\geq1$, a global lower bound certifies that either strategy's incumbent cost is at most $ε$ times the global optimum. We further demonstrate joint sensing-mode, visitation-order, and continuous-trajectory selection for a mobile-manipulator inspection task, including action precedences expressed in linear temporal logic over finite traces (LTL$_f$). Both traversal strategies find feasible solutions on all benchmark instances within 30s with mean certified optimality gaps of 28.1% and 29.7%, respectively, whereas two recent baselines succeed on only about half of the instances

</details>


### [143] [Anatomy-Informed Neural Networks: Encoding Anatomic Priors in Loss and Architecture, with an SE(3) Formulation of Guidewire-Induced Aortoiliac Deformation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21332)
*David P. Stonko*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出解剖学信息神经网络（AINN），通过软硬先验约束解决深度学习模型产生解剖学不合理预测和数据稀缺时泛化差的问题，在有限数据下实现了主动脉-髂血管树在导丝介入下的形变建模。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有深度学习解剖模型存在两大问题：1）数值合理但解剖学不可能的预测（如肾移植动脉错误起源于髂动脉而非主动脉）；2）数据稀缺时泛化能力差。这限制了其在临床应用中的可靠性，特别是在训练数据有限的场景下。

Method: 将解剖学先验分为软约束和硬约束两类：软约束（如分支惩罚项）以惩罚项形式加入损失函数，类似于物理信息神经网络；硬约束（如血管连续性）嵌入网络架构和状态表示中，从构造上排除无效预测。具体实现：将血管中心线和导丝路径从R^3提升到SE(3)李群中的标架曲线，通过Cosserat杆模型和扭曲调制建立血管-导丝耦合模型，使用单向管腔接触不等式约束。预测是耦合弹性能量的约束最小化解，接触力为拉格朗日乘子。采用Wasserstein-2最优传输损失监督预测投影与观察到的血管造影图像。

Result: 在有限临床数据下成功建模了导丝介入时主动脉-髂血管树的形变。运动学、损失函数和投影模块已针对已知真值验证；力学求解器仅针对自身最优性条件验证。预测位移尚未达到网格收敛。当前阶段未训练神经网络，仅建立了计算框架。

Conclusion: AINN框架通过解剖学先验约束有效提升了模型的可解释性和数据效率，为自主血管内导航等临床应用提供了新思路。未来工作将把该计算模型迁移至真实CT扫描数据，验证其在提高预测准确性和减少训练数据需求方面的效果。

Abstract: Deep-learning models of anatomy can be numerically plausible yet anatomically impossible, and they generalize poorly when data are scarce. We introduce Anatomy-Informed Neural Networks (AINN), in which soft anatomic priors enter as penalty terms in the loss (e.g., a branching penalty that treats a renal transplant artery off the iliac instead of the aorta as unexpected rather than impossible), in direct analogy to a physics-informed neural network, and hard anatomic priors (e.g., continuity of the vessel) are built into the architecture and state representation, making such invalid predictions impossible by construction wherever the prior admits architectural enforcement.
  We develop it on a clinical test case with limited data: how the aortoiliac tree deforms when a stiff wire is introduced endoluminally. This is important to contemporary aortic surgery and will matter to autonomous endovascular navigation. We lift the vessel centerline and the wire path from R^3 to curves of frames in the Lie group SE(3), and couple a Cosserat-rod wire to a tortuosity-modulated, anatomically anchored vessel through a unilateral lumen-contact inequality. The prediction is a constrained minimizer of the coupled elastic energy, with contact forces as its Lagrange multipliers. Supervision is a Wasserstein-2 optimal-transport loss between the predicted projection through the C-arm geometry and the observed angiogram, so a 2D angiogram can train a 3D prediction. The kinematics, loss and projection are verified against known ground truth; the mechanics solver only against its own optimality conditions, and predicted displacement is not yet mesh-converged. Here, no network is trained. Future work will transfer this in silico model to real CT scans and test whether it improves predictive accuracy and reduces the training data required.

</details>


### [144] [VIALS: A Benchmark for Visual Interpretation of Artifacts in the Life Sciences](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21357)
*Elaine Lau,Thanuka Udumulla,Lee Izhaki-Tavor,Francisco Guzmán,Nicholas Magazine,Jonas Mueller*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文引入VIALS基准测试，包含161个生物科学视觉解释任务，发现前沿视觉-语言模型无法准确解释这些科学图像，而领域专家则能轻松完成，揭示了AI在专业生命科学工作流中的局限性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在专业生命科学工作流中，科学家需要解读各种视觉产物（凝胶印迹、显微镜图像、质粒图谱、流式细胞术图、分子结构等）来指导研究决策。当前前沿视觉-语言模型虽然能流利描述自然图像，但在解释科学图像方面存在显著缺陷，这限制了AI在专业生命科学场景中的实用价值。

Method: 构建VIALS视觉问答基准，包含161个生物技术行业实验工作流中的真实视觉解释任务，涵盖整个实验流程中出现的各种视觉产物类型（而非来自出版物和教科书的精修图表），并对比前沿视觉-语言模型与领域专家的表现。

Result: 前沿视觉-语言模型无法准确解释这些科学图像，反映出其在领域知识和领域特定视觉推理能力方面的局限性。相比之下，具有相关领域专业知识的科学家认为这些视觉解释任务简单直接。

Conclusion: 无法类似地解释科学图像的AI将在专业生命科学工作流中具有有限的实用性，因为此类视觉产物是科学家推理、交流和决策的核心。这凸显了提升AI领域特定视觉理解能力的必要性。

Abstract: In professional life sciences workflows, scientists routinely interpret visual artifacts (gel blots, microscopy images, plasmid maps, flow cytometry plots, molecular structures, ...) to inform research decisions. We introduce VIALS, a visual question-answering benchmark with 161 such interpretation tasks, spanning the types of artifacts examined throughout experimental workflows in the biotech industry (rather than polished figures from publications and textbooks). While frontier vision-language models can now fluently describe natural images, we find that they are unable to accurately interpret these scientific images, reflecting limitations in domain knowledge and domain-specific visual reasoning capabilities. In contrast, scientists with relevant domain expertise find these visual interpretation tasks straightforward. AI that cannot similarly interpret such images will have limited utility in professional life sciences workflows, where such artifacts are central to how scientists reason, communicate, and make decisions.

</details>


<div id='cs.AR'></div>

# cs.AR [[Back]](#toc)

### [145] [Event-triggered Implicit Perturbation for Zeroth-Order Fine-Tuning of Spiking Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21223)
*Tengteng Lei,Prabodh Katti,Rashi Dutt,Houssem Sifaou,Tan Peng,Osvaldo Simeone,Kai Xu,Bipin Rajendran*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种隐式扰动零阶优化（IPZO）架构，通过事件触发的扰动生成单元（PGU）和地址驱动的XOR重组方案，解决了在内存计算加速器上部署零阶优化时的读写操作开销和随机数生成器硬件开销问题，实现了脉冲神经网络的高效微调。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 零阶优化仅通过前向传播估计梯度，适合微调不可微分的事件驱动脉冲神经网络，但在内存计算加速器上的部署面临两大挑战：(1)显式权重扰动导致的重复读-修改-写操作；(2)统计独立的逐权重扰动需要大量随机数生成器，硬件开销过大。

Method: 提出隐式扰动零阶优化（IPZO）架构，包含：(1)事件触发扰动生成单元（PGU），将计算出的扰动和与IMC阵列产生的加权和结合，消除扰动引起的RMW操作并保持权重静态执行；(2)利用脉冲稀疏性，PGU仅对脉冲激活的权重行生成和累积扰动贡献，减少RNG阵列的行维度需求；(3)地址驱动XOR重组方案（PGU-XOR），缓解直接RNG重用（PGU-Reuse）导致的空间相关性问题。

Result: (1) PGU-XOR在Spikingformer/CIFAR-10上达到与软件RNG匹配的精度（76.41% vs. 76.53%），在SpikeGPT/WikiText-2上达到相当的困惑度（54.20 vs. 53.23），而PGU-Reuse精度下降9.56个百分点，困惑度增加11.8；(2)基于TSMC 16nm工艺，PGU-XOR相比PGU-Reuse每次矩阵向量乘法增加40.3%-46.0%面积和15.2%-48.9%能耗开销，但更快收敛使总扰动能耗降至PGU-Reuse的0.51倍（等精度下）；(3) IPZO在批次大小B=64和时间步T=4时，将扰动能耗降至传统显式权重扰动的0.46x-0.83x，且优势随BT减小而增大。

Conclusion: IPZO架构成功解决了零阶优化在内存计算加速器上的部署瓶颈，通过消除RMW操作、利用脉冲稀疏性和引入XOR重组方案，在保持高精度的同时显著降低了硬件开销和能耗，为脉冲神经网络的高效微调提供了实用化解决方案。

Abstract: Zeroth-order (ZO) optimization estimates gradients using only forward-pass evaluations, making it suitable for fine-tuning non-differentiable, event-driven spiking neural networks (SNNs). However, its deployment on in-memory computing (IMC) accelerators is constrained by the repeated read-modify-write (RMW) operations arising from explicit weight perturbation and the prohibitive hardware footprint of random number generators (RNGs) for statistically independent per-weight perturbations. To address these challenges, we propose an implicit-perturbation ZO (IPZO) architecture in which perturbation sums computed by an event-triggered perturbation generation unit (PGU) are combined with the weighted sums produced by the IMC array, eliminating perturbation-induced RMW operations while preserving weight-stationary execution of IMC. By exploiting spike sparsity, the PGU generates and accumulates perturbation contributions only for spike-activated weight rows, reducing the required row dimension of the RNG array. An address-driven XOR recombination scheme (PGU-XOR) is further introduced to mitigate the spatial correlations caused by direct RNG reuse (PGU-Reuse). The results show that (1) PGU-XOR matches software RNGs in accuracy on Spikingformer/CIFAR-10 (76.41% vs. 76.53%) and perplexity (PPL) on SpikeGPT/WikiText-2 (54.20 vs. 53.23), whereas PGU-Reuse degrades accuracy by 9.56 percentage points and increases PPL by 11.8; (2) implemented in a TSMC 16-nm CMOS technology, PGU-XOR incurs 40.3%-46.0% area and 15.2%-48.9% energy overhead per matrix-vector multiplication relative to PGU-Reuse, yet its faster convergence reduces the total perturbation energy to 0.51x that of PGU-Reuse at iso-accuracy; (3) IPZO reduces the perturbation energy to 0.46x-0.83x that of conventional explicit weight perturbation for a batch size of B=64 and T=4 time steps, with the advantage growing as BT decreases.

</details>


### [146] [SPICE: Speculative Prefetching with Low-Rank Expert Surrogates and Heterogeneous Orchestration for MoE Inference Acceleration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21240)
*Yongxiang Lyu,Ning Li,Bonian Jia*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: SPICE是一个针对MoE模型卸载的推测性预取框架，通过结合轻量级专家预测和置信度感知的CPU-GPU协同调度，在保持模型质量的同时显著降低了推理延迟，实现了高达3.12倍的TPOT加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 混合专家模型(MoE)因其稀疏激活特性被广泛应用于大语言模型，但巨大的专家参数量往往超出GPU内存容量，导致推理延迟主要受限于主机到设备的PCIe传输时间，亟需优化专家加载机制以提升推理效率。

Method: SPICE采用两阶段策略：(1)构建与目标MoE架构对齐的轻量级草稿模型，使用置信度感知的自适应前瞻算法预取高置信度专家；(2)当预测失误时，采用成本感知的CPU-GPU异构调度：低置信度失误通过常驻共享专家和低秩专家(LoRE)代理进行近似，而精确残差工作则卸载到CPU并与GPU计算异步并行执行。

Result: 在DeepSeek-V2-Lite和Qwen2-57B-A14B模型上跨多个GPU平台进行评估，SPICE在时间每输出词元(TPOT)上实现了高达3.12倍的加速，同时质量损失极小。

Conclusion: 有效的MoE卸载不仅需要预测未来专家，还需要智能决策哪些预测失误值得近似处理、哪些需要精确恢复，以及精确残差计算应在何处执行，这对于优化MoE推理性能至关重要。

Abstract: Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) models are increasingly used in LLMs because sparse activation decouples model capacity from compute cost. However, the large expert parameter footprint often exceeds GPU memory capacity, making inference latency dominated by the host-to-device PCIe transfers for expert loading. To address these challenges, this paper presents SPICE, a speculative prefetching framework for MoE offloading that combines lightweight expert prediction with confidence-aware CPU-GPU orchestration. On one hand, SPICE builds a lightweight draft model aligned with the target MoE architecture, using a confidence-aware adaptive lookahead algorithm to prefetch high-confidence experts. On the other hand, when speculative predictions miss, SPICE switches to a cost-aware CPU-GPU heterogeneous orchestration: low-confidence misses are approximated by the resident shared expert with low rank expert (LoRE) surrogates, while exact residual work is offloaded to the CPU and executed asynchronously in parallel with ongoing GPU computation. Evaluated on DeepSeek-V2-Lite and Qwen2-57B-A14B across diverse GPU platforms, SPICE achieves up to 3.12 speedup in Time Per Output Token (TPOT) with minimal quality loss, showing that effective MoE offloading requires not only predicting future experts, but also deciding which misses deserve approximation, which require exact recovery, and where exact residual work should execute.

</details>


### [147] [Assessing Triple Modular Redundancy for Wide-Link, Low-Latency NoC Routers: Reliability and Physical Design Challenges](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21288)
*Chen Wu,Michael Rogenmoser,Luca Benini,Angelo Garofalo*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文评估了在7nm工艺下对具有512位宽链路的低延迟NoC路由器实施三重模块冗余(TMR)的成本与可靠性权衡，发现全粒度TMR能有效消除所有单粒子效应故障，且在完整的AI加速器Tile中仅增加16.8%面积和15.2%功耗。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 物理AI Tile加速器在恶劣环境中部署时，其片上网络(NoC)易受单粒子效应(SEEs)影响，可能导致死锁和静默数据损坏。现有可靠NoC研究主要针对窄链路和SEU，而先进技术节点下的超宽链路、高频低延迟NoC路由器缺乏充分的防护评估。

Method: 在TSMC 7nm工艺下完成RTL到GDSII的物理设计，针对具有512位宽链路、2周期延迟的NoC路由器，评估三种粒度（粗粒度、仅状态、全粒度）的TMR实现方案。开展RTL级和网表级的SEU及SET故障注入实验，从独立路由器扩展到完整AI加速器Tile进行系统级评估。

Result: 仅状态和粗粒度TMR无法提供足够的SEE防护；全粒度TMR在超过100万次故障注入实验中消除所有观察到的故障。独立全TMR路由器产生7.04倍面积开销，但在集成处理器和L1存储器的完整AI加速器Tile中，系统级开销降为16.8%面积和15.2%功耗，关键路径不受影响。

Conclusion: 先进技术节点（如7nm）提供了充足的布线资源，使得全粒度TMR成为保护恶劣环境下物理AI系统NoC的实用且可部署的解决方案，在系统层面的成本开销可控。

Abstract: Protecting the Network-on-Chip (NoC) of physical-AI tile-based accelerators deployed in harsh environments against single-event effects (SEEs) is paramount for preventing NoC failures that can lead to deadlocks and silent data corruption (SDC). Prior work on reliable NoCs has largely focused on narrow links (e.g., 32-bit), deeply pipelined routers, and single-event upsets (SEUs). However, the state of the art has evolved toward low-latency NoC routers with ultra-wide links, implemented on advanced technology nodes and operating at frequencies above 1 GHz. We evaluate the cost and reliability trade-offs of implementing Triple Modular Redundancy (TMR) at three granularities (coarse, state-only, and full) for a 2-cycle-latency NoC router with 512-bit wide links. We carry out RTL-to-GDSII physical design in TSMC 7nm technology, as well as both RTL- and netlist-level SEU and SET fault injection campaigns. We evaluate the three TMR approaches in terms of reliability, cost, and physical design strategies, further extending the assessment from a standalone router to a full AI acceleration tile. Our results show that state-only and coarse-grained TMR do not provide sufficient protection against SEEs, whereas full TMR eliminates all observed failures across more than one million injected faults per experiment. Although the standalone full-TMR router incurs a 7.04x area overhead, this cost is drastically amortized once integrated into a complete AI accelerator tile with processors and local L1 memories: the same design adds only 16.8% area and 15.2% power consumption under a GEMM benchmark at the system level, with the critical path of the tile entirely unaffected. These results demonstrate that advanced technology nodes provide sufficient routing capacity to make full TMR a practical and deployable solution for protecting NoCs in Physical AI systems operating in harsh environments.

</details>


<div id='cs.NI'></div>

# cs.NI [[Back]](#toc)

### [148] [Towards Traffic Modelling of Multi-Agent Systems: The Role of Coordination Topology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20494)
*Davide Lamagna,Albert Cabellos,Alberto Rodriguez-Natal,Gábor Rétvári,Berta Serracanta*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 首次对多智能体LLM系统的流量模式进行实证分析，发现拓扑结构根本性地塑造请求到达过程：扇出协调引入双峰分布，推理阶段服从对数正态分布，经典泊松模型被拒绝。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多智能体LLM系统是新兴的网络化工作负载，其内部生成请求（单个用户任务触发结构化的模型调用序列），时序由协调逻辑而非用户到达率决定。现有面向人类驱动工作负载的经典流量模型是否适用尚不清楚。

Method: 提出多层测量框架，对顺序、星型和全网格三种智能体协调拓扑进行实证表征，每种拓扑执行500次重复运行，分析LLM调用间隔时间分布。

Result: 拓扑结构根本性地塑造请求到达过程：扇出协调引入顺序执行中不存在的结构性双峰分布；推理阶段分量最好用对数正态分布描述；泊松指数零模型在所有拓扑中被明确拒绝；差异传播至推理和网络层指标。

Conclusion: 经典流量模型不适用于多智能体LLM系统，需要开发新的流量模型以准确刻画智能体协调带来的复杂请求模式。

Abstract: Multi-agent LLM systems are an emerging networked workload whose rapid deployment raises questions about the traffic patterns they generate. Compared to conventional applications, these systems generate requests internally: a single user task can induce a structured sequence of model calls whose timing is governed by coordination logic rather than by user arrival rate. It is not clear whether classical traffic models, designed for human-driven workloads, apply to this setting.
  We present an empirical characterisation of LLM-call interarrival time distributions across sequential, star, and full-mesh agentic coordination topologies, using a multi-layer measurement framework over 500 repeated runs per topology. We find that topology fundamentally shapes the arrival process of requests to the LLM backend: fan-out coordination introduces a structural bimodality absent in sequential execution, and the reasoningphase component is best described by a log-normal distribution, with the Poisson exponential null model decisively rejected across all topologies. These differences propagate to inference and network level metrics. The framework and analysis pipeline are released openly at https://github.com/dlamagna/agentraffic.

</details>


### [149] [Making Time-Sensitive Networking Deployable: A Comprehensive Lifecycle Architecture](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20500)
*Rubi Debnath,Paul Pop,Silviu S. Craciunas,Marc Boyer,Sebastian Steinhorst*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文综述了时间敏感网络（TSN）的端到端部署全生命周期，指出当前研究多聚焦单一目标优化而忽略实际硬件约束，缺乏统一的部署视角与协调研究，并总结了现有工具的局限性、关键研究空白及未来方向，为下一代可部署TSN网络提供指导。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管IEEE 802.1 TSN标准提供了完善的机制集合，但实际部署仍是复杂的多步骤工程任务。现有研究主要针对单一目标进行优化（如调度性、延迟、求解速度等），其假设往往偏离实际硬件约束，这些方法能否在真实系统中部署仍是未解之题，缺乏统一的端到端部署视角、共识与协调研究。

Method: 本文从部署视角出发，对TSN部署生命周期进行系统性综述，梳理当前挑战、现有工具的局限性，并基于端到端部署工作流程分析关键研究空白。

Result: 识别出从部署角度出发的关键研究空白，揭示了现有工具与方法论在实际部署中的局限性，明确了当前缺乏统一视角、共识和协调研究的问题，并总结了未来研究方向。

Conclusion: 为实现下一代可部署的TSN网络，需要从部署视角开展更全面的研究，弥合理论研究与实际硬件约束之间的差距，并推动统一视角与协调研究的发展。

Abstract: Time-Sensitive Networking (TSN) provides deterministic guarantees and bounded latency for safety-critical applications across different domains. While the IEEE 802.1 TSN standards provide a comprehensive set of mechanisms for time synchronization, shaping, scheduling, resource reservation, and reliability, deploying TSN in real-world applications remains a complex, multi-step engineering task. Existing research primarily focuses on optimizing individual objectives, such as increasing schedulability, reducing latency, accelerating solution generation, or improving performance analysis. However, whether these extensively researched solutions can be deployed in real-world systems remains unanswered, as the assumptions made in these methodologies often differ from practical hardware constraints. Therefore, a broader, holistic view of end-to-end TSN deployment and management remains an open research question. Currently, there is a lack of a unified perspective, consensus, and coordinated research efforts focusing on a comprehensive end-to-end TSN deployment workflow. We present a comprehensive overview of the TSN deployment lifecycle, current challenges, limitations of existing tools, and future research directions for TSN deployment and management. Lastly, we identify key research gaps from a deployment perspective and provide guidance for the development of next-generation deployable TSN networks.

</details>


### [150] [Fluid-Dynamic Interference Modeling for LEO Mega-Constellations: A Spatiotemporal Kinetic Field Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20651)
*Wen-Yu Dong,Weiwei Jiang,Song Zhao,Rui-Si Han,Qi Bi,Sheng Chen*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出了动能干扰场框架，将LEO巨型星座建模为可压缩流体壳，推导出时间演化的中断概率闭式解，发现高纬度"干扰激增"源于轨道压缩，并提出79°倾角优于传统90°极轨设计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LEO巨型星座产生高度非平稳的干扰环境，传统的静态随机几何快照方法无法准确捕捉这种动态特性，需要新的分析框架来描述星座运动对干扰的时变影响。

Method: 将星座建模为可压缩流体壳，把卫星运动映射为连续通量场，推导出聚合干扰的流体动力学守恒定律，通过矩匹配方法获得时变中断概率的闭式表达式。

Result: 揭示了高纬度"干扰激增"是轨道压缩和边界通量的直接结果；数值验证表明该框架在时间演化、纬度和设计参数方面具有高精度；发现79°倾角在低高度可实现覆盖连续性与几何干扰隔离的良好权衡。

Conclusion: 所提出的动能干扰场框架为干扰感知的6G非地面网络设计提供了可处理的分析工具，挑战了传统90°极轨设计的普适最优性。

Abstract: Low Earth orbit (LEO) mega-constellations create a highly non-stationary interference environment that cannot be accurately captured by static stochastic-geometry snapshots. This paper proposes a kinetic interference field framework that models the constellation as a compressible fluid shell evolving under orbital kinematics. By mapping satellite motion into a continuum flux field, we derive a hydrodynamic conservation law for the aggregate interference and obtain a closed-form expression for the time-varying outage probability via moment matching. The analysis reveals that high-latitude ``interference surges'' are a direct consequence of orbital compression and boundary flux, rather than random anomalies. Numerical validation against ephemeris-driven Monte Carlo simulations confirms the accuracy of the framework across time evolution, latitude, and design parameters. Leveraging the closed-form model, we further show that the conventional $90^{\circ}$ polar-orbit design is not universally outage-optimal. Instead, an inclination angle near $79^{\circ}$ at low altitude achieves a favorable trade-off between coverage continuity and geometric interference isolation. The proposed framework provides a tractable analytical tool for interference-aware 6G non-terrestrial network (NTN) design.

</details>


### [151] [Mitigating Proxy-Induced Traffic Drift in Website Fingerprinting via Model-Agnostic Traffic Tailoring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20692)
*Linxiao Yu,Tianyu Cui,Xinhao Deng,Yuqi Qing,Jun Tao,Ke Xu,Qi Li*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 提出PA3预处理框架，通过识别和消除代理协议导致的流量特征漂移，显著提升网站指纹识别模型在未知代理协议上的泛化性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于深度学习的网站指纹识别模型在面对不同代理协议时性能严重下降，原因是代理协议会改变相同网站的流量模式，导致特征漂移，而模型无法捕捉这种变化。

Method: 提出PA3模型无关预处理框架：首先识别协议特定的特征漂移指纹，然后利用这些指纹对代理流量进行定制化处理以实现特征对齐，从而缓解漂移问题。

Result: PA3平均提升F1-score 0.12（约27%相对提升），最高绝对增益达0.41，最佳情况下在未知协议流量上达到0.96以上的F1-score。

Conclusion: PA3有效缩小了代理协议漂移带来的性能差距，显著提升了WF模型在未知协议上的泛化能力，为跨协议网站指纹识别提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Website fingerprinting (WF) based on deep learning can effectively identify websites from encrypted traffic. However, users often rely on proxy protocols to bypass censorship, and the diversity of these protocols poses a major challenge, as WF models trained on traffic from one set of protocols perform poorly when evaluated on that from unseen protocols. We attribute this issue to proxy-induced feature drift, where traffic patterns of the same website vary with the proxy protocol, leading to discrepancies that WF models fail to capture and severe performance degradation. To tackle this issue, we propose PA3, a model-agnostic preprocessing framework to analyze and mitigate the proxy-induced drift. PA3 first fingerprints the protocol-specific drift. These fingerprints are then used to tailor the proxied traffic for feature alignment, which mitigates the drift and considerably improves the generalization of WF models on traffic from unseen protocols. Extensive evaluations demonstrate that PA3 substantially enhances generalization on unseen protocols with an average improvement of 0.12 in F1-score (roughly 27% relative), achieving up to a 0.41 absolute gain across models, which narrows the performance gap introduced by the drift. In the best case, PA3 enables WF models to obtain F1-scores above 0.96 on traffic from unseen protocols.

</details>


### [152] [Orchra: Stateful-aware Cross-slice Workload Migrations in the 6G Control Plane](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20893)
*Anthony Kiggundu,Bin Han,Hans D. Schotten*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出了Orchra，一种智能编排器，通过将关键UE状态（包括NAS上下文、安全密钥和PDU会话信息）外部化到临时暂存层，实现了5G-Advanced/6G网络中有状态的低延迟切片切换，将用户面中断时间相比传统3GPP方案减少一半以上。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前标准云原生5G架构缺乏对有状态的切片间/切片内会话迁移的原生支持，依赖于高开销的NAS重注册和容器重新部署等机制，导致用户面流量中断时间高达245.50毫秒，限制了网络切片在运行时进行无缝切换的能力。

Method: 提出Orchra智能编排器，将关键用户设备状态（NAS上下文、安全密钥、PDU会话信息）外部化到一个临时暂存层，在切片边界间进行状态化、低延迟的上下文传输，从而在不需要完整重注册的情况下保持会话连续性。

Result: 实验评估表明，与传统基于3GPP的方法相比，Orchra将用户面中断时间减少了超过两倍，同时引入的安全开销可以忽略不计。

Conclusion: Orchra为云原生5G-Advanced网络中实现无缝、状态保持的切片切换提供了一种实用且可复现的方法，证明了该方案的可行性和有效性。

Abstract: Network slicing is a foundational capability of Fifth Generation (5G)-Advanced and emerging Sixth Generation (6G) networks, yet practical support for seamless runtime slice transitions remains limited. Standard cloud-native 5G architectures lack native support for stateful inter/intra-slice session migration, relying instead on high-overhead Non-Access Stratum (NAS) re-registrations, container redeployment etc., which disrupt userplane traffic for up to 245.50 ms. To address this limitation, we present Orchra, an intelligent orchestrator for stateful, low-latency context transfer. By externalizing critical user equipment state-including NAS context, security keys, and Protocol Data Unit (PDU) session information-into a transient staging layer, Orchra preserves session continuity across slice boundaries without requiring full re-registration.
  Experimental evaluation shows that Orchra reduces this userplane interruption by more than twice in comparison to conventional Third Generation Partnership Project (3GPP)-based approaches while incurring negligible security overhead. These results demonstrate a practical and reproducible approach for enabling seamless, state-preserving slice transitions in cloud-native 5G-Advanced networks.

</details>


<div id='cs.DC'></div>

# cs.DC [[Back]](#toc)

### [153] [Scalpel3: A High-Performance Data Carving Architecture for Recovery of Fragmented Files](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20363)
*Karley Waguespack,Samuel Goodwin,George Hendrick,Samuel Hildebrand,Thomas Landaiche,Mingyang Li,Joshua McCain,Tyler Saizan,Jacob Tucker,James M. Ghawaly,Golden G. Richard*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了Scalpel3，首个通用、开源、大规模并行文件雕刻框架，支持连续和碎片化文件恢复，旨在降低研究门槛并推动文件雕刻技术应用于实际工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的文件雕刻工具（如Foremost、Scalpel v1/2、PhotoRec）能有效处理连续文件，部分工具在特定格式下支持碎片化恢复，但缺乏一个通用、高性能、可大规模部署的框架来支持多种文件类型的碎片化恢复策略开发与部署。

Method: 设计了一个可扩展、高性能的架构，研究人员只需开发单线程文件验证逻辑（以及可选的块验证或自定义重组逻辑），框架后端自动管理并行化、同步和快速I/O。集成了ONNX Runtime支持学习分类器参与块验证和重组，并提供持久检查点、人机交互控制和块级去重等实用功能。

Result: 成功构建了首个通用、开源、大规模并行文件雕刻框架Scalpel3，支持连续和碎片化文件恢复，具备高性能和可扩展性，并集成了机器学习能力和实用调查功能。

Conclusion: 通过公开发布该框架，旨在降低实验门槛，促进文件雕刻研究成果转化为从业者可用的实际工具，推动数字取证和数据恢复领域的发展。

Abstract: File carving recovers files from raw storage media without relying on filesystem metadata, a key  capability in digital forensics, data recovery, and digital exploration. Traditional tools such as Foremost, Scalpel v1/2, and PhotoRec handle contiguous files effectively, and some support  fragmented recovery for specific formats under simplifying assumptions, but no publicly available tool provides a general-purpose, high-performance framework for developing and deploying  fragmented recovery strategies across many file types at scale. This paper presents Scalpel3, the first general-purpose, open source, massively parallel file carving framework designed to support  both contiguous and fragmented recovery at scale. Its primary contribution is an extensible, high-performance architecture that enables researchers to develop, test, and deploy new carving  strategies without implementing their own multithreaded backends or I/O infrastructure. Adding support for new file types typically requires only development of single-threaded file validation logic and, where useful, block validation or custom reassembly logic -- the Scalpel3 backend manages parallelization, synchronization, and fast I/O automatically. The architecture also integrates the ONNX Runtime, allowing learned classifiers to participate in block validation and reassembly when useful. Beyond raw performance, Scalpel3 introduces practical features for real-world investigations: persistent checkpointing for long-running jobs, human-in-the-loop control allowing operators to monitor progress and redirect effort interactively, and block-level deduplication to shrink the search space. By making this framework publicly available, we aim to  lower barriers to experimentation and help translate file carving research into practitioner-ready tools.

</details>


### [154] [Benchmarking LLM Serving Systems for Agentic AI Workloads with XPerf](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20370)
*Michael Wang,Yikang Yue,Shaobo Li,Yirui Eric Zhou,Chen Wang,Jian Huang*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了XPerf，一个用于对LLM服务系统进行负载测试的基准测试框架，专注于多样化的智能体AI工作负载，通过细粒度轨迹重放方法解决智能体工作负载不可预测性挑战，提供详细的性能剖析与瓶颈识别能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对LLM服务系统进行智能体工作负载基准测试极具挑战性。智能体应用依赖非确定性的LLM输出来引导其控制流，导致工作负载模式在不同运行间不可预测地变化，难以进行可复现的性能评估与瓶颈定位。

Method: 提出细粒度轨迹重放方法：支持从真实智能体应用收集轨迹、按需合成具有不同模式的新工作负载、并在不同LLM服务系统上可复现地重放。框架默认包含八个覆盖编码、深度研究、问答等多样化场景的智能体应用。

Result: 实验验证XPerf能准确重放智能体工作负载，提供详细的性能分解分析，可扩展至更大规模服务系统，并能辅助服务系统调试。框架将在GitHub开源。

Conclusion: XPerf成功解决了智能体工作负载下的LLM服务系统基准测试难题，为识别性能瓶颈、优化服务系统提供了可靠工具，填补了该领域的空白。

Abstract: We present XPerf, a benchmarking framework that load-tests LLM serving systems with diverse agentic AI workloads. It provides detailed profiling of the serving system and hardware, enabling users to identify performance bottlenecks introduced by agentic workloads. Benchmarking LLM serving systems under agentic workloads is challenging - agentic applications rely on nondeterministic LLM outputs to guide their control flow; therefore, workload patterns vary unpredictably from run to run. XPerf minimizes this workload variation with a fine-grained trace replay approach: it enables users to easily collect traces from real agentic applications, synthesize new workloads with various patterns if needed, and reproducibly replay them on different LLM serving systems. XPerf includes eight agentic applications across diverse use cases (e.g., coding, deep research, and Q&A) by default. Our empirical study using these workloads shows that XPerf accurately replays agentic workloads, provides detailed performance breakdowns, scales to larger serving systems, and assists in serving system debugging. We will open-source XPerf on GitHub.

</details>


### [155] [Enabling Memory-efficient Im2win Convolution with Multi-precision Support on GPU CUDA and Tensor Cores](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20725)
*Xiang Fu,Jixiang Ma,Xinpeng Zhang,Peng Zhao,Shuai Lu,Xu Tony Liu*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文将im2win卷积范式扩展至CUDA核心和Tensor Core，通过新内核设计（如Z字形内存访问）实现高效卷积，在12个CNN基准上比cuDNN快1.4倍、比GEMM快6.4倍，内存占用仅为其53%和35%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 卷积是深度神经网络的主要计算瓶颈，现有GPU卷积方法存在内存开销大、缓存利用率低、对不同卷积核尺寸适应性差或数值不稳定等问题，亟需一种统一的高效卷积框架。

Method: 扩展im2win范式以支持CUDA核心的全精度和Tensor Core的半精度计算；引入Z字形内存访问模式和异步数据移动等新型内核设计与优化，充分利用硬件加速的半精度矩阵乘累加操作。

Result: 在12个CNN基准测试中，Tensor Core版本比CUDA核心实现高2.8倍TFLOPS，比cuDNN高1.4倍，比基于cuBLAS的GEMM卷积高6.4倍；内存占用仅为cuDNN的53%、cuBLAS的35%。

Conclusion: im2win确立了作为现代GPU架构统一、高性能卷积框架的地位，有效解决了现有方法的内存与性能问题。

Abstract: Convolution is a principal computational bottleneck in deep neural networks, and its efficiency depends on tight integration between algorithms and GPU hardware. Existing GPU convolution methods suffer from large memory overhead, poor cache utilization, limited effectiveness across kernel sizes, or numerical instability. This work extends the im2win paradigm -- a universal, memory-efficient convolution method with contiguous memory access for all kernel sizes -- to run efficiently in full precision on CUDA cores and half precision on tensor cores. By introducing new kernel designs and optimizations such as zig-zag memory access and asynchronous data movement, im2win efficiently exploits hardware-accelerated half-precision matrix multiply-accumulate operations. Across twelve CNN benchmarks, im2win achieves up to 2.8x higher TFLOPS than its CUDA core implementation, 1.4x higher than cuDNN, and 6.4x higher than GEMM-based convolution with cuBLAS, while using as little as 53% and 35% of their memory, respectively. These results establish im2win as a unified, high-performance convolution framework for modern GPU architectures.

</details>


### [156] [MEMPOWER: Efficient Power Management with Fine-grained Memory Analysis and Modeling for HPC Workloads](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20734)
*Nanda Velugoti,Joseph Manzano,Andres Marquez,Nathan Tallent,Kyle Hale*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出MEMPOWER框架，通过细粒度内存行为感知的动态电压/频率调节，在HPC并行应用中实现6%-42%的能耗降低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 并行应用的能耗管理是HPC和云计算环境的重要问题。随着应用不断挑战现代机器的内存墙，计算与数据移动之间的不平衡日益加剧，为智能调节CPU功耗提供了新机会。然而现有的频率和电压缩放技术无法充分捕捉内存访问行为的细粒度变化，导致计算/数据访问不平衡对能够利用它的系统组件不可见，从而错失潜在节能机会。

Method: 提出MEMPOWER——一种灵活的、基于模型的方法。该方法通过表征并行工作负载的细粒度内存行为来暴露计算/数据移动不平衡。基于此表征，构建自动化软件框架，静态检测应用二进制文件，并根据模型确定的电压/频率转换来平衡内存访问行为的细粒度变化与硬件转换开销。

Result: 在一系列HPC基准测试上，MEMPOWER相比标准OS/硬件管理的功耗控制机制，在执行时间影响最小的情况下，实现了6%到42%的EDP（能耗延迟积）降低。

Conclusion: MEMPOWER成功证明了通过细粒度内存行为感知的模型驱动方法能够有效识别并利用计算/数据移动不平衡，实现显著的能耗优化，为HPC和云计算环境中的并行应用功耗管理提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Managing the energy consumption and power efficiency of parallel applications is a significant issue in both HPC environments and in the cloud. As emerging applications continue to push against the memory wall of modern machines, the growing imbalance between compute and data movement creates new opportunities to intelligently tune CPU power consumption. Unfortunately, existing frequency and voltage scaling techniques do not adequately capture fine-grained changes in memory access behavior, rendering the compute/data access imbalance invisible to the components of the system that could capitalize on it, thus leaving potential power savings on the table. In this paper, we propose MEMPOWER, a flexible, model-based approach to exposing compute/data movement imbalance that characterizes the fine-grained memory behavior of parallel workloads. This characterization then informs our automated software framework which can statically instrument the application binary with model-determined voltage/frequency transitions that balance fine-grained changes in memory access behavior with the costs of hardware transitions. Using MEMPOWER, we demonstrate a reduction in EDP of 6% to 42% on a range of HPC benchmarks with minimal impact on execution time when compared to the standard OS/hardware-managed power control mechanism.

</details>


### [157] [PRICE: Pricing-based Resource Incentives for Quality-of-Result-aware Computing at the Edge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20819)
*Uwe Gropengießer,Sebastian Frenz,Max Mühlhäuser*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: PRICE是一种基于定价的激励机制，通过利用边缘计算中请求质量的可调节性来应对过载问题，显著提升了资源利用效率和吞吐量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 边缘节点受限于设计容量，但许多边缘工作负载可以在运行时权衡结果质量与资源效率。现有的边缘定价机制大多将请求视为固定配置提交，很少在过载情况下利用每个请求的质量灵活性。

Method: 提出PRICE激励机制，将利用率相关的价格信号与每个请求的质量选择相结合。随着利用率的增加，接受价格的上涨使得资源密集型变体不太可能被选择，从而将接受的请求转向更轻量的执行，使节点在接近容量的情况下服务更多请求。

Result: 在真实硬件上持续过载的评估中，PRICE在接受吞吐量和CPU利用率方面优于固定分配和动态定价基准。结果在定价函数族、任务持续时间和客户端群体方面具有稳健性。

Conclusion: 结果质量灵活性是边缘过载管理中一个强大但未被充分利用的控制维度，而定价是利用这一维度的有效机制。

Abstract: Edge nodes are capacity-constrained by design, yet many edge workloads can trade result quality for resource efficiency at runtime. Existing edge pricing mechanisms largely treat requests as fixed-configuration submissions and rarely exploit per-request quality flexibility under overload. We present PRICE, an incentive mechanism that couples a utilization-dependent price signal to per-request quality selection. As utilization increases, rising acceptance prices make resource-intensive variants less likely to be selected, shifting accepted requests toward lighter execution and allowing the node to serve significantly more requests while operating near capacity. Evaluation on real hardware under sustained overload shows that PRICE outperforms both fixed-allocation and dynamic-pricing baselines in accepted throughput and CPU utilization. The results are robust across pricing function families, task-duration distributions, and client populations. Result-quality flexibility is a powerful but underused control dimension for overload management at the edge, and pricing is an effective mechanism to exploit it.

</details>


### [158] [AI Infrastructure in Space: How Far Can We Go?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21034)
*Qing Li,Qiyang Zhang,Daliang Xu,Tianze Huang,Dingge Zhang,Yihao Zhao,Xiaolong Huang,Jinfeng Wen,Xiameng Hu,Tao Qi,Mengwei Xu,Shangguang Wang,Xuanzhe Liu*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了太空AI基础设施的系统愿景，通过三个在轨案例研究表明：卫星可用算力受热能约束限制，应用更新传输延迟可显著降低，且有状态VLM服务需要处理热中断导致的执行状态恢复问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着卫星成为可编程计算平台，能够运行日益增长的AI工作负载，出现了系统层面的挑战：当计算能力、连接性、能源和热裕度在轨道运行期间发生变化时，如何确保AI服务在发射后仍可部署、可管理和可恢复？

Method: 定义太空AI基础设施为管理跨航天器、轨道网络、地面站和云后端的AI能力的系统层，将轨道和物理状态视为资源模型的一部分。综合地面AI基础设施、卫星网络和卫星边缘计算的相关基础，并检验直接影响系统设计的物理约束。通过三个在轨案例研究（节点级BUPT-1卫星、平台级BUPT-2卫星的SateLight、服务级有状态VLM）验证该愿景。

Result: BUPT-1卫星遥测数据显示可用计算容量受热能包络约束；BUPT-2卫星上的SateLight将应用更新传输延迟平均降低56.54%，最高降低91.18%，更新正确率达100%；有状态VLM服务案例显示热中断使执行状态恢复成为一等系统问题。

Conclusion: 这些发现为太空原生资源管理、生命周期支持和跨天地持续AI服务的研究议程提供了动机，推动建立能够适应轨道动态变化的太空AI基础设施。

Abstract: Satellites are becoming programmable computing platforms capable of running increasingly demanding AI workloads. This shift raises a systems problem: how can AI services remain deployable, manageable, and recoverable after launch when compute capacity, connectivity, energy, and thermal headroom vary over orbital time? This paper develops a systems vision for AI infrastructure in space. We define it as the systems layer that manages AI capabilities across spacecraft, orbital networks, ground stations, and cloud backends, while treating orbital and physical state as part of the resource model. We synthesize relevant foundations from terrestrial AI infrastructure, satellite networking, and satellite edge computing, and examine the physical constraints that directly shape system design. We further ground this vision in three in-orbit case studies spanning the node, platform, and service levels. Telemetry from BUPT-1 satellite shows that usable compute capacity is bounded by thermal and energy envelopes. SateLight on BUPT-2 satellite reduces application-update transmission latency by 56.54% on average and up to 91.18%, with 100% update correctness. A stateful VLM serving case further shows that thermal interruptions make execution-state recovery a first-class systems problem. These observations motivate a research agenda for space-native resource management, lifecycle support, and sustained AI service across space and ground.

</details>


### [159] [Integrating a Python Dynamical core into ICON](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21150)
*Mauro Bianco,Till Ehrengruber,Enrique González Paredes,Andreas Jocksch,Christos Kotsalos,Ioannis Magkanaris,Philip Müller,Edoardo Paone,Mikael Simberg,Hannes Vogt,Jacopo Canton,Yilu Chen,Anurag Dipankar,Nicoletta Farabullini,Michael Jähn,Matthieu Leclair,Ong Chia Rui,Nathan Beech,Nicolas Gruber,Christoph Müller,Daniel Hupp,Xavier Lapillonne*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文成功将Python动态核心集成到Fortran气候模拟代码中，在生成级全球模拟中实现了20-30%的性能提升，证明了Python可以提供高效、可持续且硬件无关的气候建模解决方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 地球系统模型向百亿亿次计算过渡面临僵化的单体Fortran代码库和维护密集型编译器指令的阻碍。虽然高级DSL提供了解决方案，但由于集成困难而经常失败，亟需一种无缝集成方法。

Method: 基于GT4Py DSL和Data-Centric (DaCe)优化框架，将基于Python的ICON动态核心集成到原始Fortran模拟代码中，通过高级数据流优化和自动内核融合，从单一可移植Python源生成优化的设备代码。

Result: 在生产级全球模拟中，Python动态核心相比高度优化的Fortran+OpenACC实现实现了20-30%的性能提升，在耦合设置中总体时间提升10%。这挑战了Python编排会带来不可接受的HPC开销的假设。

Conclusion: 该工作证明了Python可以为全球气候建模提供可持续、高效且硬件无关的未来，通过高级数据流优化和自动内核融合，用硬件无关的代码生成替代了硬件纠缠的指令。

Abstract: The transition of Earth-system models to exascale is often hindered by rigid, monolithic Fortran codebases and maintenance-heavy compiler directives. While high-level DSLs offer a solution, they frequently fail due to cumbersome integration. We present the integration of a Python-based ICON dynamical core into the original Fortran simulation code. Leveraging the GT4Py DSL and the Data-Centric (DaCe) optimization framework, we demonstrate that high-level Python can be seamlessly integrated into legacy infrastructure without performance loss. Our results challenge the assumption that Python orchestration introduces prohibitive HPC overhead. In production-grade global simulations, our Python dynamical core achieves a 20--30\% performance improvement over the highly-optimized Fortran+OpenACC implementation, with a 10\% improvement on the total time for a coupled setup. Driven by advanced data-flow optimizations and automated kernel fusion, this approach replaces hardware-entangled directives by generating optimized device code from a single, portable Python source. This work proves that Python can provide a sustainable, efficient, and hardware-agnostic future for global climate modeling.

</details>


### [160] [HIERA: Workload-Aware Planning Across Implementation Spaces for GPU Kernel Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21157)
*Jinghao Wang,Qiqi Gu,Chenpeng Wu,Jianguo Yao,Haibing Guan,Xijun Li*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 提出HIERA框架，通过分层搜索空间规划实现GPU内核的自动化生成与优化，在KernelBench上优于现有无训练方法，且在科学计算算子上超越cuDNN 1.53倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着工作负载多样化和GPU硬件快速演进，自动化的GPU内核生成与优化变得日益重要。现有基于LLM的方法通常在固定实现空间内进行优化，限制了优化灵活性或搜索效率。

Method: HIERA是一种分层搜索空间规划框架，构建契约增强的任务规范，在PyTorch算子、CUDA库和自定义CUDA内核之间选择适当的实现空间，并利用性能分析反馈和专家知识指导结构化的迭代优化。

Result: 在KernelBench上的多级别工作负载和基础LLM实验中，HIERA相比现有无训练方法具有更强的实现有效性、样本效率和优化性能，与需要训练的CUDA-L1保持竞争力而无需额外模型训练。在科学计算专用模板算子的案例研究中，相比cuDNN实现1.53倍加速。

Conclusion: HIERA为GPU内核优化提供了一个有效的分层搜索空间规划方案，在保持竞争力的同时避免了额外的模型训练成本，并展示了在标准机器学习工作负载之外的通用潜力。

Abstract: High-performance GPU kernels underpin modern deep learning and scientific computing. As workloads become increasingly diverse and GPU hardware evolves rapidly, developing efficient methods for automated GPU kernel generation and optimization has become increasingly important. Existing LLM-based methods typically optimize within a fixed implementation space, limiting either optimization flexibility or search efficiency. We propose \textsc{HIERA}, a hierarchical search-space planning framework for GPU kernel optimization. \textsc{HIERA} constructs contract-augmented task specifications, selects an appropriate implementation space across PyTorch operators, CUDA libraries, and custom CUDA kernels, and uses profiling feedback and expert knowledge to guide structured iterative refinement. Experiments on KernelBench across multiple various workload levels and base LLMs show that \textsc{HIERA} delivers stronger overall implementation validity, sample efficiency, and optimization performance than existing training-free methods, while remaining competitive with the training-based CUDA-L1 without additional model training. A case study on a specialized stencil operator from scientific computing further achieves a \(1.53\times\) speedup over cuDNN, demonstrating the potentiality of the general framework beyond standard machine-learning workloads.

</details>


### [161] [GrAND: GPU-based Dynamic Graph Indexes for Approximate Nearest Neighbour Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21163)
*Karthik Venkatasubba,Shivendra Deshpande,Shivram S,Jyothi Vedurada*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 提出了GrAND，一种GPU原生动态图索引方法，支持高效的并发更新操作，在真实数据集上相比现有方法提升2.2x-25.4倍吞吐量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代近似最近邻搜索应用需要在不断演化的向量集合上工作，要求图索引在保持高召回率的同时支持高吞吐量搜索和插入/删除操作。然而，现有GPU图索引多为静态或更新支持有限，更新操作（邻居发现、反向边创建、剪枝、删除修复）的并发执行会产生冗余距离计算和共享邻接表的访问冲突。

Method: 提出GrAND（GPU-based Dynamic Graph Indexes），针对Vamana和CAGRA两种流行图索引实现了GPU原生动态更新算法。主要技术包括：(1)批量整合图修复以消除冗余剪枝计算；(2)采用无锁查找-替换策略实现并行邻接表更新；(3)在GPU上按需构建反向图进行可靠的原地删除，无需永久复制索引。

Result: 在7个真实数据集和5种流式工作负载上评估，相比SVFusion和FreshDiskANN-GPU，GrAND分别提升整体工作负载吞吐量2.2x-8.7x和6.5x-25.4x，同时在持续更新下保持高搜索吞吐量和召回率。

Conclusion: GrAND成功解决了GPU图索引的动态更新挑战，通过批量处理、无锁并行和按需反向图构建等技术，实现了高效并发更新，显著优于现有GPU动态索引方案。

Abstract: Modern Approximate Nearest Neighbour Search (ANNS) applications operate over continuously evolving vector collections and require graph indexes that sustain high-throughput searches while incorporating insertions and deletions with high recall. However, most GPU graph indexes are static or provide limited update support. Updates require neighbour discovery, reverse-edge creation, pruning, and deletion-induced graph repair; executing these operations concurrently introduces redundant distance computations and conflicting accesses to shared adjacency lists. Background-rebuild-based deletion further incurs substantial computation, additional memory consumption, and interference with foreground queries.
  We present GrAND (GPU-based Dynamic Graph Indexes for Approximate Nearest Neighbour Search), a GPU-native collection of dynamic-update algorithms for two popular graph indexes, Vamana and CAGRA. GrAND consolidates graph repair across a batch, eliminating redundant pruning computations, and employs a lock-free find-and-replace strategy for parallel adjacency-list updates. For reliable in-place deletion, GrAND constructs an on-demand reverse graph on the GPU, accurately identifying incoming edges without permanently duplicating the index. We evaluate GrAND on seven real-world datasets across five streaming workloads, comparing it against SVFusion and FreshDiskANN-GPU (our GPU adaptation of FreshDiskANN). GrAND improves overall workload throughput by 2.2x-8.7x and 6.5x-25.4x, respectively, while maintaining high search throughput and recall over sustained updates.

</details>
